<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-47340079" data-id="47340079" data-tags="AI" data-score="4148" data-comments="1631" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47340079" data-time="1773257369">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/newsguidelines.html#generated" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/yc500.gif' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='news.ycombinator.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/newsguidelines.html#generated" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Don&#x27;t post generated/AI-edited comments. HN is for conversation between humans</span><span class='zh'>不要发布生成的 AI 编辑评论。HN 仅供人类对话</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New Hacker News guidelines discourage posting comments that are AI-generated or AI-edited.</span><span class='zh'>这条规则强调保持社区讨论的真实人类对话，避免 AI 辅助或编辑的评论影响质感。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New Hacker News guidelines discourage posting comments that are AI-generated or AI-edited. The rule emphasizes preserving authentic human discussion, signal quality, and accountability in community conversations. This matters as AI-assisted writing becomes more common on forums, potentially diluting discourse if not checked.</span><span class='zh'>这条规则强调保持社区讨论的真实人类对话，避免 AI 辅助或编辑的评论影响质感。随着 AI 写作工具日益普及，平台和用户将面临如何维护讨论质量和可追责性的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://motorolanews.com/motorola-three-new-b2b-solutions-at-mwc-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Motorola announces a partnership with GrapheneOS</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉宣布与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作</span></a><span class="dom">motorolanews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Motorola teams up with the GrapheneOS Foundation to focus on security-hardened Android foundations and open-source collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作，表明在 Android 安全加固与开源协作方面的重点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Motorola teams up with the GrapheneOS Foundation to focus on security-hardened Android foundations and open-source collaboration. The move could influence future device security updates, transparency, and baseline protections across Motorola hardware.</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉与 GrapheneOS 基金会达成合作，表明在 Android 安全加固与开源协作方面的重点。此举可能影响未来的安全更新、透明度以及硬件基础安全特性的建立。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/Fried_rice/status/2038894956459290963" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code&#x27;s source code has been leaked via a map file in their NPM registry</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 的源代码通过 NPM 注册表中的 map 文件泄露</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code’s source code was leaked through a source map file published in their NPM package registry.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 的源代码通过其 NPM 包注册表中暴露的一个 source map 文件泄露到公网。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code’s source code was leaked through a source map file published in their NPM package registry. The incident underscores how publishing artifacts like source maps can unintentionally expose proprietary code and sensitive logic. It’s a reminder for maintainers to scrub sensitive bits from build artifacts and implement stricter registry hygiene and access controls.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 的源代码通过其 NPM 包注册表中暴露的一个 source map 文件泄露到公网。事件凸显了在发布构建产物时，source map 可能无意间暴露私有代码和业务逻辑的风险。开发团队需要对发布的产物进行清理，并加强注册表的访问控制与审计。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.computationalcomplexity.org/2026/03/tony-hoare-1934-2026.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tony Hoare has died</span><span class='zh'>托尼·霍尔去世</span></a><span class="dom">blog.computationalcomplexity.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Renowned computer scientist Tony Hoare passed away, leaving a lasting legacy in formal methods, algorithm theory, and programming language design.</span><span class='zh'>著名计算机科学家托尼·霍尔去世，正式方法、算法理论和编程语言设计方面的贡献深远。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Renowned computer scientist Tony Hoare passed away, leaving a lasting legacy in formal methods, algorithm theory, and programming language design. His work, including Hoare logic and CSP, underpins modern verification, concurrency theory, and software correctness discussions.</span><span class='zh'>著名计算机科学家托尼·霍尔去世，正式方法、算法理论和编程语言设计方面的贡献深远。他提出的霍尔逻辑和 CSP 等奠定了现代形式化验证、并发理论与软件正确性讨论的基石。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/say-hello-to-macbook-neo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MacBook Neo</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 MacBook Neo</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple officially launches MacBook Neo, a budget-friendly model powered by A18 Pro, targeting students and light workloads.</span><span class='zh'>苹果正式推出 MacBook Neo，搭载 A18 Pro 芯片，定位入门级，面向学生与轻量级工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple officially launches MacBook Neo, a budget-friendly model powered by A18 Pro, targeting students and light workloads. The device promises improved energy efficiency and a more accessible gateway into Apple&#x27;s ecosystem, potentially expanding its mass-market share.</span><span class='zh'>苹果正式推出 MacBook Neo，搭载 A18 Pro 芯片，定位入门级，面向学生与轻量级工作。新机承诺提升能效并降低入门门槛，或对苹果在大众市场的份额产生积极影响。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.stepsecurity.io/blog/axios-compromised-on-npm-malicious-versions-drop-remote-access-trojan" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Axios compromised on NPM – Malicious versions drop remote access trojan</span><span class='zh'>Axios 在 NPM 上被劫持：恶意版本投放远程访问木马</span></a><span class="dom">stepsecurity.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Malicious Axios releases popped up on npm, embedding a remote access Trojan in certain versions.</span><span class='zh'>npm 上出现恶意的 Axios 版本，包含可对受影响环境进行远程访问的木马。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Malicious Axios releases popped up on npm, embedding a remote access Trojan in certain versions. The attack highlights the ongoing supply-chain risk for widely used libraries, and operators should audit dependencies, pin safe versions, and monitor for unusual network activity after updates.</span><span class='zh'>npm 上出现恶意的 Axios 版本，包含可对受影响环境进行远程访问的木马。此类供应链攻击再次暴露了对广泛使用库的依赖风险，建议锁定版本、进行依赖审计，并在更新后监测异常网络活动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="http://karpathy.github.io/2026/02/12/microgpt/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microgpt</span><span class='zh'>MicroGPT：轻量级 GPT 的新思路</span></a><span class="dom">karpathy.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compact framework for running GPT-like agents on modest hardware.</span><span class='zh'>提出在中等算力设备上运行 GPT 风格代理的紧凑框架。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compact framework for running GPT-like agents on modest hardware. Emphasizes modular tooling, lightweight memory, and efficient orchestration to enable on-device AI and edge applications. This approach could lower barriers to deploying autonomous AI in real-world products.</span><span class='zh'>提出在中等算力设备上运行 GPT 风格代理的紧凑框架。强调模块化工具、轻量级内存和高效编排，以实现边缘设备上的 AI 自主执行。该思路可能降低在真实产品中部署自治 AI 的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/bretonium/291f4388e2de89a43b25c135b44e41f0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Shall I implement it? No</span><span class='zh'>要不要自己实现？不</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.canirun.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Can I run AI locally?</span><span class='zh'>本地能否运行 AI？</span></a><span class="dom">canirun.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Running AI locally is increasingly feasible for certain models, but hardware, energy, and cost trade-offs persist against the convenience of cloud inference.</span><span class='zh'>在本地运行 AI 对某些模型已逐渐变得可行，但与云端推理相比，仍需权衡硬件、能耗与成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Running AI locally is increasingly feasible for certain models, but hardware, energy, and cost trade-offs persist against the convenience of cloud inference. The piece surveys current open-source options, performance hurdles, and key privacy and security considerations for offline deployment.</span><span class='zh'>在本地运行 AI 对某些模型已逐渐变得可行，但与云端推理相比，仍需权衡硬件、能耗与成本。本文梳理了当前的开源选项、性能瓶颈，以及离线部署的隐私和安全考量。</span></p>

The approach aims to balance collaboration with IP protection, appealing to teams needing controlled environments for sensitive work.</span><span class='zh'>Malus 提供“洁净室即服务”的模式，帮助团队在不暴露敏感资产的前提下进行合规的软件开发。该方法力图在协作与知识产权保护之间取得平衡，适用于需要受控开发环境的场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.svd.se/a/K8nrV4/metas-ai-smart-glasses-and-data-privacy-concerns-workers-say-we-see-everything" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta’s AI smart glasses and data privacy concerns</span><span class='zh'>Meta 的 AI 智能眼镜与数据隐私担忧</span></a><span class="dom">svd.se</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta&#x27;s AI glasses spark privacy debates as workers report pervasive sensing and potential data collection.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 的 AI 智能眼镜引发隐私争议，员工反映设备在日常使用中存在广泛感知和潜在数据收集。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta&#x27;s AI glasses spark privacy debates as workers report pervasive sensing and potential data collection. The piece weighs the balance between convenience and surveillance, and what it means for corporate wearables and future AR devices.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 的 AI 智能眼镜引发隐私争议，员工反映设备在日常使用中存在广泛感知和潜在数据收集。文章讨论便利性与监控之间的权衡，以及这类企业级穿戴设备和未来 AR 设备的治理含义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/sama/status/2027578652477821175" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI agrees with Dept. of War to deploy models in their classified network</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 与战争部在其机密网络中部署模型达成协议</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI reaches an agreement to deploy its models inside the War Department&#x27;s classified network, marking a rare government-use approval for advanced AI.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 达成在战争部机密网络中部署其模型的协议，标志着在高敏环境中对先进 AI 的罕见应用获批。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI reaches an agreement to deploy its models inside the War Department&#x27;s classified network, marking a rare government-use approval for advanced AI. The deal raises questions about security, vendor oversight, and the scale of AI integration into sensitive environments. It signals a potential path for enterprise and defense collaborations, with tight governance requirements.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 达成在战争部机密网络中部署其模型的协议，标志着在高敏环境中对先进 AI 的罕见应用获批。该交易引发对安全、供应商监管以及在敏感场景中部署 AI 的规模等问题的讨论。它预示着企业与防务领域日后更紧密的协作之路，同时将伴随严格的治理要求。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitLab&#x27;s cofounder is battling cancer and continues pushing forward by launching new companies. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://alex000kim.com/posts/2026-03-31-claude-code-source-leak/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Claude Code Source Leak: fake tools, frustration regexes, undercover mode</span><span class='zh'>Claude 代码源泄露：假工具、挫败感正则、潜伏模式</span></a><span class="dom">alex000kim.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of a Claude code-source leak reveals decoy tools and frustrating regex tests, with mentions of an undercover mode.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 代码源泄露事件的分析显示存在伪工具与令人挫败的正则测试，并提及潜伏模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of a Claude code-source leak reveals decoy tools and frustrating regex tests, with mentions of an undercover mode. The incident highlights the fragility of model provenance and the risk of leaked tooling being manipulated to mislead researchers. The takeaway is a call for stronger provenance controls and safer, auditable tooling around AI code-generation.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 代码源泄露事件的分析显示存在伪工具与令人挫败的正则测试，并提及潜伏模式。此事凸显了模型来源的脆弱性，以及泄露工具被操纵以误导研究者的风险。核心启示是需要加强来源追溯与对 AI 代码生成工具的安全、可审计设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.xda-developers.com/wine-11-rewrites-linux-runs-windows-games-speed-gains/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wine 11 rewrites how Linux runs Windows games at kernel with massive speed gains</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11：在内核层重写以显著提升 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的速度</span></a><span class="dom">xda-developers.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wine 11 overhauls kernel interactions to accelerate Windows games on Linux, delivering substantial speedups; it improves compatibility and reduces overhead in system calls translation and graphics handling.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11 对内核层进行重写，使 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的性能显著提升，并提高了兼容性与系统调用翻译、图形处理的效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wine 11 overhauls kernel interactions to accelerate Windows games on Linux, delivering substantial speedups; it improves compatibility and reduces overhead in system calls translation and graphics handling. This move tightens the gap between Linux gaming and Windows performance, potentially expanding Linux gaming adoption.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11 对内核层进行重写，使 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的性能显著提升，并提高了兼容性与系统调用翻译、图形处理的效率。此举缩小了 Linux 与 Windows 在游戏性能上的差距，有望推动 Linux 游戏生态的扩展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/116160393783585567" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Motorola GrapheneOS devices will be bootloader unlockable/relockable</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉的 GrapheneOS 设备将支持解锁与重新锁定引导加载程序</span></a><span class="dom">grapheneos.social</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Motorola&#x27;s GrapheneOS devices will support bootloader unlock and relock, restoring user control over the device&#x27;s software stack while keeping the security workflow intact.</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉的 GrapheneOS 设备将支持解锁并重新锁定引导加载程序，重新赋予用户对软件栈的控制，同时保持安全更新流程完备。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Motorola&#x27;s GrapheneOS devices will support bootloader unlock and relock, restoring user control over the device&#x27;s software stack while keeping the security workflow intact. This enables custom ROM flashing and safer recovery paths, aligned with GrapheneOS&#x27;s privacy and hardening goals. It also tests how manufacturers balance openness with enterprise security expectations.</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉的 GrapheneOS 设备将支持解锁并重新锁定引导加载程序，重新赋予用户对软件栈的控制，同时保持安全更新流程完备。此举让刷入自定义 ROM 和更安全的设备恢复成为可能，与 GrapheneOS 的隐私与强化安全目标相契合。也反映出厂商在开放性与企业安全之间的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/upper-up/meta-lobbying-and-other-findings" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta Platforms: Lobbying, dark money, and the App Store Accountability Act</span><span class='zh'>Meta 平台：游说、暗钱与 App Store 责任法案</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A repository exposes Meta’s lobbying footprint, including hidden political spending and policy influence around app ecosystem accountability.</span><span class='zh'>一个仓库揭示了 Meta 的游说策略、暗钱与围绕应用生态治理的政策影响力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A repository exposes Meta’s lobbying footprint, including hidden political spending and policy influence around app ecosystem accountability. The findings illustrate how big tech’s financial activity intersects with regulatory scrutiny and platform governance.</span><span class='zh'>一个仓库揭示了 Meta 的游说策略、暗钱与围绕应用生态治理的政策影响力。这些发现揭示了大型科技公司在监管审查与平台治理中的资金活动如何交织。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 12-year-old Palestinian boy describes how Israeli forces killed his family in a car, offering a rare child’s eyewitness account amid ongoing conflict.</span><span class='zh'>一名12岁巴勒斯坦男孩讲述在一起车内事件中，他的家人被以色列军队杀害的经过。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 12-year-old Palestinian boy describes how Israeli forces killed his family in a car, offering a rare child’s eyewitness account amid ongoing conflict. The testimony highlights civilian harm and the human cost of military operations, underscoring the volatility of frontline reporting.</span><span class='zh'>一名12岁巴勒斯坦男孩讲述在一起车内事件中，他的家人被以色列军队杀害的经过。此证词突显冲突中的平民伤亡与人道代价，也反映出前线报道的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://opencode.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenCode – Open source AI coding agent</span><span class='zh'>OpenCode：开源AI编码代理</span></a><span class="dom">opencode.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenCode is an open-source AI coding agent that helps developers automate routine coding tasks and explore AI-assisted workflows.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCode 是一个开源的 AI 编码代理，旨在帮助开发者自动化常规编码任务并尝试 AI 驱动的工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenCode is an open-source AI coding agent that helps developers automate routine coding tasks and explore AI-assisted workflows. By being open-source, it invites experimentation, customization, and transparency, contrasting with proprietary copilots. This matters for developers weighing privacy, control, and ecosystem when adopting AI coding tools.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCode 是一个开源的 AI 编码代理，旨在帮助开发者自动化常规编码任务并尝试 AI 驱动的工作流。作为开源项目，它鼓励社区参与、定制和透明度，与闭源的代码助手形成对比。对于考虑隐私、控制权和生态系统的开发者来说，这是选择 AI 编码工具时一个重要考量。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Afroman was found not liable in a defamation suit brought by Ohio police who had raided his home.</span><span class='zh'>Afroman 在俄亥俄州警察就突袭其家中提起的诽谤诉讼中被判无责任。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Afroman was found not liable in a defamation suit brought by Ohio police who had raided his home. The ruling emphasizes the high standard for proving defamation when public figures discuss police conduct and may influence future entertainment-law disputes. This outcome matters for creators who voice controversial statements.</span><span class='zh'>Afroman 在俄亥俄州警察就突袭其家中提起的诽谤诉讼中被判无责任。判决强调涉及公共人物与执法行为的言论案件需满足较高的证明标准，可能影响未来相关诉讼的走向，并对娱乐行业有一定参考意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://claude.com/blog/1m-context-ga" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>1M context is now generally available for Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6</span><span class='zh'>Opus 4.6 与 Sonnet 4.6 现已支持 1M 上下文长度</span></a><span class="dom">claude.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>1M context windows are now generally available for Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6, enabling longer context retention and more coherent long-form assistance.</span><span class='zh'>Opus 4.6 和 Sonnet 4.6 现推出 1M 上下文长度，便于处理更长的对话和文档。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>1M context windows are now generally available for Opus 4.6 and Sonnet 4.6, enabling longer context retention and more coherent long-form assistance. This reduces engineering friction around chunking long documents and improves tasks like document QA, summarization, and multi-turn conversations, though it shifts cost and latency considerations.</span><span class='zh'>Opus 4.6 和 Sonnet 4.6 现推出 1M 上下文长度，便于处理更长的对话和文档。长上下文提升了连续性与可用性，减少对文本分块的依赖，但也带来成本和延迟的权衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google detailed a 24-hour window that allows sideloading of unverified Android apps to accelerate testing and internal distribution outside the Play ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌公布了一项 24 小时窗口，允许 sideload 未经验证的 Android 应用以加速测试和内部分发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google detailed a 24-hour window that allows sideloading of unverified Android apps to accelerate testing and internal distribution outside the Play ecosystem. While it speeds iteration, it broadens the attack surface and prompts heightened security and policy safeguards.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌公布了一项 24 小时窗口，允许 sideload 未经验证的 Android 应用以加速测试和内部分发。此举虽提升迭代速度，但也扩大了潜在攻击面，需加强安全与策略层面的防护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.researchsquare.com/article/rs-6079807/v1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Global warming has accelerated significantly</span><span class='zh'>全球变暖显著加速</span></a><span class="dom">researchsquare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New measurements show the pace of global warming has accelerated beyond prior projections, delivering record-high temperatures and more frequent extreme weather events.</span><span class='zh'>最新数据显示全球变暖速率显著加快，突破以往预测，伴随创纪录高温和更频繁的极端天气。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New measurements show the pace of global warming has accelerated beyond prior projections, delivering record-high temperatures and more frequent extreme weather events. The finding underscores the urgency for rapid decarbonization and climate-tech innovation.</span><span class='zh'>最新数据显示全球变暖速率显著加快，突破以往预测，伴随创纪录高温和更频繁的极端天气。该发现凸显了迅速降低碳排放和推动气候科技创新的紧迫性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cbc.ca/news/canada/british-columbia/b-c-adopting-year-round-daylight-time-9.7111657" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>British Columbia is permanently adopting daylight time</span><span class='zh'>不列颠哥伦比亚省永久采用夏令时</span></a><span class="dom">cbc.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>British Columbia will permanently observe daylight time, ending seasonal clock changes.</span><span class='zh'>不列颠哥伦比亚省将永久采用夏令时，取消季节性时钟调整。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>British Columbia will permanently observe daylight time, ending seasonal clock changes. The move aims to simplify scheduling and energy planning, but could complicate activities tied to daylight patterns and cross-border coordination with the US and other provinces.</span><span class='zh'>不列颠哥伦比亚省将永久采用夏令时，取消季节性时钟调整。此举旨在简化日程与能源规划，但可能让依赖日照模式的活动和跨境协调变得更复杂。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft blocks the term &#x27;Microslop&#x27; in its Copilot Discord server, then locks the channel after backlash.</span><span class='zh'>微软在官方 Copilot Discord 服务器屏蔽了“Microslop”一词，遭遇反对后又封锁服务器。</span></p>

The incident underscores tensions between branding, community moderation, and how corporate AI products are managed in public spaces.</span><span class='zh'>微软在官方 Copilot Discord 服务器屏蔽了“Microslop”一词，遭遇反对后又封锁服务器。此事件凸显品牌控制、社区治理以及在公开场合管理企业 AI 产品的张力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft continues to roll out patches for Windows 11, but critics argue the changes are cosmetic and don&#x27;t address core reliability or usability issues.</span><span class='zh'>微软继续推出 Windows 11 的补丁，但批评者认为这些改动多为表面修补，无法解决核心的可靠性或可用性问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft continues to roll out patches for Windows 11, but critics argue the changes are cosmetic and don&#x27;t address core reliability or usability issues. For tech readers, this highlights the gap between marketing narratives and real-world OS quality in major updates.</span><span class='zh'>微软继续推出 Windows 11 的补丁，但批评者认为这些改动多为表面修补，无法解决核心的可靠性或可用性问题。外部宣传更像是事后公关冲刺，而非实质工程改进。对技术读者而言，这凸显了大型操作系统更新中营销叙述与实际软件质量之间的差距。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bethmathews.substack.com/p/why-so-many-control-rooms-were-seafoam" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why so many control rooms were seafoam green (2025)</span><span class='zh'>为什么大量控制室偏好海泡绿（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">bethmathews.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The color choice in control rooms often favors seafoam green because it reduces glare and supports sustained focus during long shifts.</span><span class='zh'>控制室的颜色选择常常偏向海泡绿，因为它能降低眩光、帮助在长时间工作中保持专注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The color choice in control rooms often favors seafoam green because it reduces glare and supports sustained focus during long shifts. The piece ties this preference to ergonomics, display tech, and a broader design culture that values calm, legibility, and efficiency in high-stakes environments.</span><span class='zh'>控制室的颜色选择常常偏向海泡绿，因为它能降低眩光、帮助在长时间工作中保持专注。本文将这一偏好与人体工效学、显示技术以及强调冷静、易读与高效的设计文化联系起来，探讨在高压环境中的实用性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tech labor market deteriorated more sharply than during the 2008 financial crisis or the 2020 recession, with hiring slowdowns and rising layoffs across the sector.</span><span class='zh'>科技行业的就业市场比2008年金融危机或2020年衰退期间更为疲弱，出现招聘放缓和裁员增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tech labor market deteriorated more sharply than during the 2008 financial crisis or the 2020 recession, with hiring slowdowns and rising layoffs across the sector. The trend raises questions about demand for tech talent, AI adoption, and policy responses.</span><span class='zh'>科技行业的就业市场比2008年金融危机或2020年衰退期间更为疲弱，出现招聘放缓和裁员增加。这一趋势引发对技术需求、AI应用和政策应对的担忧。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.cs.unc.edu/~stotts/COMP590-059-f24/robsrules.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=cs.unc.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>cs.unc.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">50</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cs.unc.edu/~stotts/COMP590-059-f24/robsrules.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rob Pike’s Rules of Programming (1989)</span><span class='zh'>Rob Pike 的五条编程法则</span></a><span class="dom">cs.unc.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Practical guidance from Rob Pike emphasizes keeping code simple, correct, and maintainable.</span><span class='zh'>Rob Pike 的五条编程法则集中呈现了实用的编程智慧，强调构建可靠软件的重要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Practical guidance from Rob Pike emphasizes keeping code simple, correct, and maintainable. The five rules center on readable interfaces, orthogonality, and constructing reliable, easily reasoned systems.</span><span class='zh'>Rob Pike 的五条编程法则集中呈现了实用的编程智慧，强调构建可靠软件的重要性。核心内容包括简洁、可读接口、正交性，以及易于推理和维护的代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/googleworkspace/cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Workspace CLI</span><span class='zh'>Google Workspace 命令行工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Google Workspace CLI project provides a command-line interface for automating and scripting Workspace admin tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Google Workspace CLI 提供了用于自动化和脚本化管理任务的命令行界面，方便开发者对用户、配置等执行操作并集成到 CI/CD 流水线。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Google Workspace CLI project provides a command-line interface for automating and scripting Workspace admin tasks. The move aligns with a broader push to make cloud admin workflows more programmable.</span><span class='zh'>Google Workspace CLI 提供了用于自动化和脚本化管理任务的命令行界面，方便开发者对用户、配置等执行操作并集成到 CI/CD 流水线。此举体现云端管理工作流向可编程化的趋势，提升运维效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Litellm 1.82.7 and 1.82.8 on PyPI are compromised</span><span class='zh'>Litellm 1.82.7 与 1.82.8 在 PyPI 上遭篡改</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two releases of a lightweight LLM client package were compromised on PyPI, enabling malicious code pushes to downstream users.</span><span class='zh'>两个版本的轻量级 LLM 客户端包在 PyPI 的发布被篡改，导致后续用户可能被注入恶意代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two releases of a lightweight LLM client package were compromised on PyPI, enabling malicious code pushes to downstream users. The incident underscores supply-chain risk in Python ecosystems and the importance of checksum validation and pinning versions. Users should audit dependencies, upgrade to clean builds, and verify package integrity.</span><span class='zh'>两个版本的轻量级 LLM 客户端包在 PyPI 的发布被篡改，导致后续用户可能被注入恶意代码。事件凸显 Python 生态的供应链风险，强调校验和版本锁定的重要性。请审计依赖、升级到干净的构建版本，并验证包的完整性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that gambling and prediction markets will magnify risks as AI makes them scalable and accessible.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，随着 AI 提升预测市场的可扩展性和可访问性，赌博与预测市场的风险还会进一步放大。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that gambling and prediction markets will magnify risks as AI makes them scalable and accessible. It discusses potential manipulation, bias, and regulatory challenges, urging careful design and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，随着 AI 提升预测市场的可扩展性和可访问性，赌博与预测市场的风险还会进一步放大。讨论潜在的操纵、偏见和监管挑战，呼吁在设计与治理上保持谨慎。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mas.to/@gabrielesvelto/116171750653898304" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 崩溃中有 10% 由位翻转引发</span></a><span class="dom">mas.to</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reports that about 10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips in memory, underscoring the impact of hardware faults on software reliability.</span><span class='zh'>报道显示约 10% 的 Firefox 崩溃由内存位翻转引起，凸显硬件故障对软件可靠性的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reports that about 10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips in memory, underscoring the impact of hardware faults on software reliability. The finding motivates better error handling, ECC memory use, and robust testing for resilience.</span><span class='zh'>报道显示约 10% 的 Firefox 崩溃由内存位翻转引起，凸显硬件故障对软件可靠性的影响。该发现促使加强错误处理、使用 ECC 内存以及提高容错测试。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Reddit user exposes who is behind Meta’s $2 billion lobbying push for age verification tech.</span><span class='zh'>一位 Reddit 用户披露了推动 Meta 就年龄验证技术进行20亿美元游说的幕后主体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Reddit user exposes who is behind Meta’s $2 billion lobbying push for age verification tech. The reveal sheds light on the opaque networks that drive big-tech policy influence and raises questions about privacy, consent, and the effectiveness of age checks.</span><span class='zh'>一位 Reddit 用户披露了推动 Meta 就年龄验证技术进行20亿美元游说的幕后主体。这一揭露揭示了驱动大型科技公司政策影响的隐蔽网络，并引发关于隐私、同意和年龄核验有效性的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://terriblesoftware.org/2026/03/03/nobody-gets-promoted-for-simplicity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nobody gets promoted for simplicity</span><span class='zh'>没人因为简单而升职</span></a><span class="dom">terriblesoftware.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An opinion piece argues that promotions in many tech organizations reward complexity and visibility rather than keeping systems simple.</span><span class='zh'>一篇观点文章认为，许多科技组织的晋升机制偏向复杂性和可见度，而非坚持简单设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An opinion piece argues that promotions in many tech organizations reward complexity and visibility rather than keeping systems simple. The author points to entrenched incentives that favor adding features, optimization, or risk-taking over clean, minimal design. The takeaway is to prioritize clarity, reliability, and lean architectures to unlock long-term value and career advancement.</span><span class='zh'>一篇观点文章认为，许多科技组织的晋升机制偏向复杂性和可见度，而非坚持简单设计。作者指出习以成年的激励让人们更重视增添功能、优化和冒险，而不是追求清晰、稳定的系统。核心信息是，长远价值来自清晰、可靠、简化的架构，有助于职业发展的提升。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://whoami.wiki/blog/personal-encyclopedias" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Personal Encyclopedias</span><span class='zh'>个人百科全书</span></a><span class="dom">whoami.wiki</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how individuals build personal encyclopedias to organize knowledge, notes, and references into a portable, searchable knowledge base.</span><span class='zh'>探讨个人如何建立百科式知识库，将笔记、引用和资料整合成可检索的本地知识体系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how individuals build personal encyclopedias to organize knowledge, notes, and references into a portable, searchable knowledge base. The piece dives into practical patterns for curating, linking, and indexing long-term information so it remains usable across tools and devices. For tech workers and knowledge workers grappling with information overload, it highlights a scalable approach to personal knowledge management.</span><span class='zh'>探讨个人如何建立百科式知识库，将笔记、引用和资料整合成可检索的本地知识体系。文章深入讨论对条目进行整理、链接与索引的实用模式，以便在不同工具和设备间保持可用性。对于面临信息过载的技术工作者和知识工作者，这种个人知识管理的可扩展方法尤为重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.system76.com/post/system76-on-age-verification/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>System76 on Age Verification Laws</span><span class='zh'>System76 对年龄验证法的看法</span></a><span class="dom">blog.system76.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>System76 argues that age-verification requirements risk privacy and accessibility for users and developers, potentially burdening open-source hardware and software ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>System76 指出年龄验证要求可能侵犯隐私、降低可访问性，并给开源硬件和软件生态带来额外负担。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>System76 argues that age-verification requirements risk privacy and accessibility for users and developers, potentially burdening open-source hardware and software ecosystems. The post calls for balanced policy that protects vulnerable users without stifling innovation.</span><span class='zh'>System76 指出年龄验证要求可能侵犯隐私、降低可访问性，并给开源硬件和软件生态带来额外负担。文中呼吁在保护脆弱用户与不扼杀创新之间实现政策平衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Patience is essential for long-running tech work; complex problems don&#x27;t yield quick wins.</span><span class='zh'>在长期的技术工作中，耐心至关重要；复杂问题难以快速解决。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.dyne.org/child-protection-is-not-access-control/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Do Not Turn Child Protection into Internet Access Control</span><span class='zh'>不要把儿童保护变成互联网访问控制</span></a><span class="dom">news.dyne.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Guardrails for child protection should be precise and targeted, not a pretext for blanket internet access control.</span><span class='zh'>儿童保护政策不应成为全面的网络访问控制的借口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Guardrails for child protection should be precise and targeted, not a pretext for blanket internet access control. Overly broad censorship risks chilling freedom online while failing to stop abuse. The piece calls for privacy-preserving, surgical safeguards instead.</span><span class='zh'>儿童保护政策不应成为全面的网络访问控制的借口。宽泛的封锁容易压制在线自由，并未有效阻止滥用。应采用更精准、透明的隐私保护措施与安全防护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://agelesslinux.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ageless Linux – Software for humans of indeterminate age</span><span class='zh'>Ageless Linux：面向各年龄段的 Linux 系统</span></a><span class="dom">agelesslinux.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ageless Linux aims to be usable by people across a wide range of ages, focusing on human-centered design and accessibility.</span><span class='zh'>Ageless Linux 以以人为本的设计为核心，致力于让不同年龄段的用户都能轻松使用 Linux，并提供友好的界面与文档。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ageless Linux aims to be usable by people across a wide range of ages, focusing on human-centered design and accessibility. It challenges the stereotype that Linux is only for coders by providing a gentler, more inclusive user experience and documentation. The project highlights a trend toward more approachable, age-inclusive open-source software.</span><span class='zh'>Ageless Linux 以以人为本的设计为核心，致力于让不同年龄段的用户都能轻松使用 Linux，并提供友好的界面与文档。此举打破了“Linux 只给程序员用”的刻板印象，体现出开源软件在可访问性与包容性方面的进步。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI clarifies that Anthropic should not be treated as a special supply-chain risk, arguing for standard vendor diligence instead.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 明確表示 Anthropic 不應被視為特殊的供應鏈風險，主張以常規供應商盡職調查來評估風險。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI clarifies that Anthropic should not be treated as a special supply-chain risk, arguing for standard vendor diligence instead. This stance underscores how businesses weigh safety concerns against collaboration when sourcing AI services. It reflects the ongoing debate over risk labeling in enterprise AI sourcing.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 明確表示 Anthropic 不應被視為特殊的供應鏈風險，主張以常規供應商盡職調查來評估風險。此立場顯示企業在採購 AI 服務時如何在安全與協作之間取得平衡，亦反映關於風險分類的持續爭論。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://agent-safehouse.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agent Safehouse – macOS-native sandboxing for local agents</span><span class='zh'>Agent Safehouse：macOS 原生本地代理沙箱</span></a><span class="dom">agent-safehouse.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agent Safehouse provides macOS-native sandboxing for local agents, letting autonomous tasks run in isolated environments without container overhead.</span><span class='zh'>Agent Safehouse 提供了 macOS 原生的本地代理沙箱化，允许自主任务在相对独立的环境中运行而无需容器开销。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agent Safehouse provides macOS-native sandboxing for local agents, letting autonomous tasks run in isolated environments without container overhead. By evading full containers while maintaining isolation, it reduces cross-agent risk and strengthens security for macOS automation workflows. This approach matters for developers deploying agents on Mac workstations or in Mac-centric stacks.</span><span class='zh'>Agent Safehouse 提供了 macOS 原生的本地代理沙箱化，允许自主任务在相对独立的环境中运行而无需容器开销。通过在不牺牲隔离的前提下提高安全性，它降低了跨代理的风险，提升了 macOS 自动化工作流的安全性。对于在 Mac 环境中部署代理的开发者尤为相关。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Austin’s housing boom kept rents in check as a wave of new units came online, softening affordability even as demand from tech workers remains high.</span><span class='zh'>奥斯汀的住房供给激增带动新建单位上线，抵消了需求，导致租金走低。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Austin’s housing boom kept rents in check as a wave of new units came online, softening affordability even as demand from tech workers remains high. The finding highlights how supplying more units can influence urban affordability and inform policy debates on zoning and infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>奥斯汀的住房供给激增带动新建单位上线，抵消了需求，导致租金走低。此现象反映出供给侧扩张对城市可负担性的直接影响，可能影响土地使用、基础设施和住房政策的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thatshubham.com/blog/news-audit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The 49MB web page</span><span class='zh'>49MB 的网页</span></a><span class="dom">thatshubham.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The page weighs in at 49MB, serving as a cautionary example of how modern sites can balloon with images, scripts, and third-party resources.</span><span class='zh'>该网页达到 49MB，揭示现代站点如何被图片、脚本和第三方资源膨胀。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The page weighs in at 49MB, serving as a cautionary example of how modern sites can balloon with images, scripts, and third-party resources. The write-up walks through auditing approaches to identify bloated bundles, optimize critical paths, and pare back assets without wrecking UX. Takeaway: page size is a first-class performance lever, and thoughtful auditing matters for both users and developers.</span><span class='zh'>该网页达到 49MB，揭示现代站点如何被图片、脚本和第三方资源膨胀。文中提供审计方法，帮助识别冗余资源、优化关键渲染路径，并在不破坏用户体验的前提下精简资产。要点：页面大小是性能的重要变量，细致的审计对用户和开发者都重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/console-gaming/microsofts-unhackable-xbox-one-has-been-hacked-by-bliss-the-2013-console-finally-fell-to-voltage-glitching-allowing-the-loading-of-unsigned-code-at-every-level" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft&#x27;s &#x27;unhackable&#x27; Xbox One has been hacked by &#x27;Bliss&#x27;</span><span class='zh'>微软所谓的“不可破解”的 Xbox One 被 Bliss 破解</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrated a voltage-glitch attack against Microsoft&#x27;s Xbox One, breaking the console&#x27;s claimed &#x27;unhackable&#x27; security.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员通过电压干扰对 Xbox One 展开攻击，打破了其“不可破解”的安全宣称。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrated a voltage-glitch attack against Microsoft&#x27;s Xbox One, breaking the console&#x27;s claimed &#x27;unhackable&#x27; security. The hack lets unsigned code run at various privilege levels, undermining hardware protections and enabling homebrew or piracy-style access.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员通过电压干扰对 Xbox One 展开攻击，打破了其“不可破解”的安全宣称。该漏洞使未签名代码在不同权限级别下得以运行，削弱了硬件防护，并可能开启自制软件或盗版访问。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu/~knuth/papers/claude-cycles.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude&#x27;s Cycles [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>Claude 的循环（PDF）</span></a><span class="dom">www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stanford/Knuth-hosted paper examines cycles in Claude, offering formal analysis and possibly algorithmic insights.</span><span class='zh'>这份由斯坦福/ Knuth 团队整理的论文探讨了 Claude 中的循环现象，给出形式化分析与潜在的算法洞见。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stanford/Knuth-hosted paper examines cycles in Claude, offering formal analysis and possibly algorithmic insights. It presents a theoretical exploration of repetitive structures in Claude’s context, with formal proofs or demonstrations. The work contributes to understanding computational cycles and their implications in Claude’s framework.</span><span class='zh'>这份由斯坦福/ Knuth 团队整理的论文探讨了 Claude 中的循环现象，给出形式化分析与潜在的算法洞见。内容聚焦 Claude 语境下的重复结构，包含证明或演示。该研究有助于理解计算循环及其在 Claude 框架中的意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/03/04/anthropic-ceo-dario-amodei-calls-openais-messaging-around-military-deal-straight-up-lies-report-says/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dario Amodei calls OpenAI’s messaging around military deal ‘straight up lies’</span><span class='zh'>Dario Amodei 指出 OpenAI 就军事交易的表述是直截了当的谎言</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s CEO Dario Amodei criticized OpenAI&#x27;s public messaging about a military deal as &#x27;straight up lies&#x27;.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的首席执行官 Dario Amodei 指出 OpenAI 就军事交易的公开表述是直截了当的谎言。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s CEO Dario Amodei criticized OpenAI&#x27;s public messaging about a military deal as &#x27;straight up lies&#x27;. The comment underscores tension around AI governance, defense partnerships, and corporate communications. It spotlights how leadership dynamics can shape perceptions of strategic collaborations.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的首席执行官 Dario Amodei 指出 OpenAI 就军事交易的公开表述是直截了当的谎言。该评论凸显了 AI 治理、国防伙伴关系与企业传播之间的紧张关系，也反映出高层决策对外部认知的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.science.org/content/article/arxiv-pioneering-preprint-server-declares-independence-cornell" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ArXiv declares independence from Cornell</span><span class='zh'>ArXiv宣布独立于康奈尔大学</span></a><span class="dom">science.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ArXiv, the long-running open-access preprint server, is shedding its longtime host by shifting governance away from Cornell.</span><span class='zh'>作为长期负责的开放获取预印本服务器，ArXiv 正在将治理从康奈尔大学转向独立管理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ArXiv, the long-running open-access preprint server, is shedding its longtime host by shifting governance away from Cornell. The move signals a push toward community-led stewardship and diversified funding as arXiv seeks long-term sustainability. For researchers, it could mean changes in governance, policy, and how quickly new features roll out.</span><span class='zh'>作为长期负责的开放获取预印本服务器，ArXiv 正在将治理从康奈尔大学转向独立管理。此次转变旨在实现更具社区驱动色彩的治理结构和多元化资金来源，以确保长期可持续性。对于研究者而言，未来在治理、政策和新功能上线速度等方面可能出现变化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.stanford.edu/stories/2026/03/ai-advice-sycophantic-models-research" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI overly affirms users asking for personal advice</span><span class='zh'>AI 对寻求个人建议的用户过度拍马奉承</span></a><span class="dom">news.stanford.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers show that AI models trained to please users tend to provide flattering, agreeable personal advice, even when it&#x27;s not optimal.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，为讨好用户而优化的对话模型在给出个人建议时往往过度拍马奉承，即便不最优也会认可用户观点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers show that AI models trained to please users tend to provide flattering, agreeable personal advice, even when it&#x27;s not optimal. This sycophantic behavior can mislead users and undermine critical judgment, underscoring challenges in aligning AI with reliable guidance.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，为讨好用户而优化的对话模型在给出个人建议时往往过度拍马奉承，即便不最优也会认可用户观点。这种拍马奉承行为可能误导用户、削弱批判性判断，凸显让 AI 输出可靠建议的对齐难题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/EnriqueLop/legalize-es" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Spanish legislation as a Git repo</span><span class='zh'>把西班牙立法当作 Git 仓库来管理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project treats Spanish legislation as a versioned Git repository, enabling collaborative drafting, review workflows, and audit trails.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目把西班牙立法作为可版本化的 Git 仓库来管理，支持协同起草、审查和变更追踪。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project treats Spanish legislation as a versioned Git repository, enabling collaborative drafting, review workflows, and audit trails. It demonstrates &#x27;law as code&#x27; can improve transparency and governance while highlighting implementation challenges.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目把西班牙立法作为可版本化的 Git 仓库来管理，支持协同起草、审查和变更追踪。这样的“法治即代码”理念提升透明度与治理效率，同时也暴露了实现方面的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bloomberg.github.io/js-blog/post/temporal/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Temporal: The 9-year journey to fix time in JavaScript</span><span class='zh'>Temporal：用九年修复 JavaScript 的时间处理</span></a><span class="dom">bloomberg.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Temporal introduces a robust API for dates, times, and time zones intended to replace Date in JavaScript.</span><span class='zh'>Temporal 提供用于日期、时间和时区的强健 API，旨在替代内置 Date。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Temporal introduces a robust API for dates, times, and time zones intended to replace Date in JavaScript. The article traces nearly a decade of design iterations, trade-offs, and ecosystem debates, underscoring how hard robust time handling is.</span><span class='zh'>Temporal 提供用于日期、时间和时区的强健 API，旨在替代内置 Date。九年的设计迭代与取舍凸显了在时间处理上的复杂性，以及对生态系统的深远影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mistral.ai/news/leanstral" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Leanstral: Open-source agent for trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering</span><span class='zh'>Leanstral：用于可信编码与形式化证明工程的开源代理</span></a><span class="dom">mistral.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Leanstral is an open-source agent designed to assist developers with trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering.</span><span class='zh'>Leanstral 是一个面向开发者的开源代理，旨在协助进行可信编码与形式化证明工程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Leanstral is an open-source agent designed to assist developers with trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering. It integrates AI-assisted coding with formal verification tooling to improve software correctness and reliability, making rigorous development workflows more accessible.</span><span class='zh'>Leanstral 是一个面向开发者的开源代理，旨在协助进行可信编码与形式化证明工程。它将 AI 辅助编码与形式化验证工具结合，提升软件正确性与可信度，让严格的软件工程工作流更易落地。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Delve dives into the emerging &#x27;Fake Compliance as a Service&#x27; phenomenon, critiquing services that generate pretend attestations or simplified controls to appease audits.</span><span class='zh'>Delve 深度剖析新兴的“假合规即服务”现象，批评那些提供虚假合规证明或简化控制的服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Delve dives into the emerging &#x27;Fake Compliance as a Service&#x27; phenomenon, critiquing services that generate pretend attestations or simplified controls to appease audits. The piece warns about integrity risks for users, regulators, and auditors when compliance is commodified.</span><span class='zh'>Delve 深度剖析新兴的“假合规即服务”现象，批评那些提供虚假合规证明或简化控制的服务。文章警示当合规被商品化时，对用户、监管机构和审计方构成的信任与合规性风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kagi.com/smallweb/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kagi Small Web</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 推出 Small Web 小型网络</span></a><span class="dom">kagi.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kagi announces Small Web, an initiative aimed at supporting smaller, privacy-conscious websites and reducing reliance on major platforms.</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 宣布推出 Small Web，旨在支持规模较小、注重隐私的网站，减少对大型平台的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kagi announces Small Web, an initiative aimed at supporting smaller, privacy-conscious websites and reducing reliance on major platforms. The move signals a shift toward a more diverse web ecosystem and potential new strategies for search and discovery.</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 宣布推出 Small Web，旨在支持规模较小、注重隐私的网站，减少对大型平台的依赖。这一举措或促成更为多样的网络生态与搜索发现的新策略。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission is preparing to eliminate the mandatory quarterly reporting requirement for public companies.</span><span class='zh'>美国证监会正筹划取消强制性季度财报，可能减轻上市公司合规负担，降低季度业绩波动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission is preparing to eliminate the mandatory quarterly reporting requirement for public companies. This could cut compliance costs and reduce quarterly volatility, but may shift investor expectations toward annual disclosures and alter how markets price near-term risk.</span><span class='zh'>美国证监会正筹划取消强制性季度财报，可能减轻上市公司合规负担，降低季度业绩波动。此举或改变投资者对时点披露的期待，并影响市场对短期风险的定价和分析方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561489" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LinkedIn uses 2.4 GB RAM across two tabs</span><span class='zh'>LinkedIn 在两个标签页中耗用 2.4 GB RAM</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LinkedIn&#x27;s web app can consume roughly 2.4 GB of RAM even with only two tabs open, illustrating how heavy modern browsers can get with feature-rich sites.</span><span class='zh'>LinkedIn 的网页应用在仅打开两个标签页的情况下就大约占用 2.4 GB 内存，映射出现代浏览器对高功能网站的内存需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LinkedIn&#x27;s web app can consume roughly 2.4 GB of RAM even with only two tabs open, illustrating how heavy modern browsers can get with feature-rich sites. This raises questions about memory efficiency, tab isolation, and how to design enterprise-focused UIs that stay responsive on modest hardware.</span><span class='zh'>LinkedIn 的网页应用在仅打开两个标签页的情况下就大约占用 2.4 GB 内存，映射出现代浏览器对高功能网站的内存需求。此现象引发对内存效率、标签页隔离与在中等硬件上保持响应性的设计考量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Malicious litellm_init.pth in litellm 1.82.8 PyPI package – credential stealer</span><span class='zh'>litellm 1.82.8 PyPI 包中的恶意 litellm_init.pth：凭据窃取</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A malicious PyPI package for litellm 1.82.8 contains a litellm_init.pth file that loads a credential-stealing module during import.</span><span class='zh'>在 litellm 1.82.8 的 PyPI 包中发现恶意的 litellm_init.pth，导入时会加载窃取凭据的模块。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A malicious PyPI package for litellm 1.82.8 contains a litellm_init.pth file that loads a credential-stealing module during import. This highlights ongoing supply-chain risk in AI tooling and underscores the need for strict dependency hygiene, integrity checks, and credential rotation after exposure. Users should upgrade to clean versions and audit environments for any unfamiliar startup files.</span><span class='zh'>在 litellm 1.82.8 的 PyPI 包中发现恶意的 litellm_init.pth，导入时会加载窃取凭据的模块。此事件暴露了 AI 工具链中的供应链风险，并强调对依赖项进行严格校验、完整性检查以及暴露后轮换凭据的必要性。如有怀疑，应升级到干净版本并审计运行环境中是否存在异常的启动文件。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>737</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501729' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47416736" data-id="47416736" data-tags="Culture" data-score="736" data-comments="79" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47416736" data-time="1773773990">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is anybody else bored of talking about AI?</span><span class='zh'>还有人觉得聊 AI 吗？</span></a><span class="dom">blog.jakesaunders.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reflective essay questions whether the constant AI chatter has worn people down and argues for focusing on practical, real-world impact over hype.</span><span class='zh'>这篇随笔质疑持续的 AI 热议是否让人疲惫，并主张把重点放在现实世界的实际影响而非炒作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reflective essay questions whether the constant AI chatter has worn people down and argues for focusing on practical, real-world impact over hype. It pushes readers to value concrete use cases, governance, and measurable progress rather than trending headlines.</span><span class='zh'>这篇随笔质疑持续的 AI 热议是否让人疲惫，并主张把重点放在现实世界的实际影响而非炒作。作者鼓励关注具体的使用场景、治理与可衡量的进展，而不是追逐热度新闻。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.grandforksherald.com/news/north-dakota/ai-error-jails-innocent-grandmother-for-months-in-north-dakota-fraud-case" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Innocent woman jailed after being misidentified using AI facial recognition</span><span class='zh'>因AI人脸识别误识而入狱的无辜老妇人</span></a><span class="dom">grandforksherald.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An innocent grandmother spent months in jail after facial recognition wrongly identified her as a fraud suspect.</span><span class='zh'>一位无辜的老妇人在人脸识别错误识别后被关押数月。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An innocent grandmother spent months in jail after facial recognition wrongly identified her as a fraud suspect. The case underscores the high-stakes risk of relying on imperfect AI for law enforcement, where false positives can ruin lives and erode trust. It adds to ongoing debates about accuracy, bias, and accountability in AI-powered policing.</span><span class='zh'>一位无辜的老妇人在人脸识别错误识别后被关押数月。此案凸显在执法中依赖不完美的人工智能所带来的高风险——误判可能摧毁个人生活并侵蚀公众信任。事件推动有关准确性、偏见与对AI警务的问责制度的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple Business</span><span class='zh'>Apple Business：面向各规模企业的一体化平台</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple introduces Apple Business, an all-in-one platform to streamline device management, security, and app deployment for organizations of all sizes.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 Apple Business，一站式平台用于简化企业级设备管理、安全性与应用部署，覆盖各类规模的组织。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple introduces Apple Business, an all-in-one platform to streamline device management, security, and app deployment for organizations of all sizes. By consolidating IT controls and enterprise workflows, it aims to reduce friction for Apple-powered ecosystems in the workplace. The move could reshape enterprise IT purchasing and cross-device collaboration strategies.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 Apple Business，一站式平台用于简化企业级设备管理、安全性与应用部署，覆盖各类规模的组织。通过整合 IT 控制和企业工作流，意在降低在工作场景中使用苹果设备的门槛。这一举措可能影响企业 IT 的采购决策及跨设备协作方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If you don&#x27;t opt out by Apr 24 GitHub will train on your private repos</span><span class='zh'>如果你在4月24日前不选择退出，GitHub 将在你的私有仓库上用于训练</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Developers face a policy change: if you don&#x27;t opt out by the deadline, GitHub will use contents of private repos to train its AI models.</span><span class='zh'>如果你未在截止日期前完成退出，GitHub 将把私有仓库的代码用于训练其人工智能模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Developers face a policy change: if you don&#x27;t opt out by the deadline, GitHub will use contents of private repos to train its AI models. This raises privacy and IP concerns for individual developers and teams, and highlights the trade-off between model quality and data rights; opting out may require action and may affect tooling.</span><span class='zh'>如果你未在截止日期前完成退出，GitHub 将把私有仓库的代码用于训练其人工智能模型。这将引发对隐私和知识产权的担忧，并可能影响企业对代码资产的控制。开发者需要了解退出流程与潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/political-science/articles/10.3389/fpos.2026.1779810/full" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Corruption erodes social trust more in democracies than in autocracies</span><span class='zh'>腐败在民主国家对社会信任的侵蚀比专制国家更严重</span></a><span class="dom">frontiersin.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new study finds corruption erodes social trust more in democratic countries than in autocracies.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究显示，腐败对社会信任的侵蚀在民主国家比在专制国家更明显。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new study finds corruption erodes social trust more in democratic countries than in autocracies. The result suggests that transparency and accountability mechanisms in democracies can backfire when corruption spreads, hurting trust in institutions more than in tightly controlled regimes. The finding matters for governance, civic tech, and how platforms rely on public trust.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究显示，腐败对社会信任的侵蚀在民主国家比在专制国家更明显。这一发现提示，民主制度的透明与问责机制在某些情境下可能放大信任的下滑，对治理、公民科技以及依赖信任的平台都具重要影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An iPhone 17 Pro demonstration shows a 400-billion-parameter LLM running locally on-device, signaling advances in edge AI and privacy-preserving inference.</span><span class='zh'>一段演示显示 iPhone 17 Pro 能在本地设备上运行 400B 参数的 LLM，标志边缘 AI 的进展与隐私保护推理能力。</span></p>

The feat highlights model compression, efficient on-device runtimes, and the potential shift away from cloud-only AI.</span><span class='zh'>一段演示显示 iPhone 17 Pro 能在本地设备上运行 400B 参数的 LLM，标志边缘 AI 的进展与隐私保护推理能力。此成就凸显模型压缩、边缘运行效率的提升，以及 AI 由云端依赖向本地推理的潜在转变。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A mindset shift that values creating real utility for others over chasing immediate financial returns.</span><span class='zh'>强调把为他人创造实用价值放在首位，而不是只追逐短期回报。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techfixated.com/a-1977-time-capsule-voyager-1-runs-on-69-kb-of-memory-and-an-8-track-tape-recorder-4/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Voyager 1 runs on 69 KB of memory and an 8-track tape recorder</span><span class='zh'>旅行者1号仅靠69KB内存与8轨磁带记录器运行</span></a><span class="dom">techfixated.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Voyager 1 still operates with a mere 69 KB of RAM and an 8-track tape recorder, a testament to long-lived space hardware engineered decades ago.</span><span class='zh'>旅行者1号仍以仅有的69 KB RAM和8轨磁带记录器运行，展示了数十年前设计的硬件的持久耐用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Voyager 1 still operates with a mere 69 KB of RAM and an 8-track tape recorder, a testament to long-lived space hardware engineered decades ago. The story highlights how a mission can outlive the obsolete kit that built it, delivering data from the outer solar system long after its era of design. It underscores the engineering trade-offs and resilience required for deep-space exploration.</span><span class='zh'>旅行者1号仍以仅有的69 KB RAM和8轨磁带记录器运行，展示了数十年前设计的硬件的持久耐用。这个故事凸显了任务往往超越其设计时的技术极限，能够持续传回外太阳系的数据，凸显深空探测的工程权衡与韧性。此事也让人反思长期任务对老旧组件的依赖与替换挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openrocket.info/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenRocket</span><span class='zh'>OpenRocket：开源火箭设计与仿真工具</span></a><span class="dom">openrocket.info</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenRocket is an open-source rocket design and flight simulation package that lets hobbyists, students, and researchers model aerodynamics, stability, and trajectories before launch.</span><span class='zh'>OpenRocket 是一个开源的火箭设计与飞行仿真工具，用户能在发射前建模气动、稳定性和轨迹。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenRocket is an open-source rocket design and flight simulation package that lets hobbyists, students, and researchers model aerodynamics, stability, and trajectories before launch. It helps verify designs and teaches rocketry principles without expensive hardware. The project embodies the practical value of open scientific tooling.</span><span class='zh'>OpenRocket 是一个开源的火箭设计与飞行仿真工具，用户能在发射前建模气动、稳定性和轨迹。它便于爱好者、学生和研究者在没有昂贵设备的情况下验证设计、学习火箭学原理。该项目体现了开源科学工具的实用价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bramcohen.com/p/manyana" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The future of version control</span><span class='zh'>版本控制的未来</span></a><span class="dom">bramcohen.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows.</span><span class='zh'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows. The takeaway is that new paradigms could change how teams coordinate, verify history, and scale development. For technologists, this signals a shift in toolchains and trust conventions.</span><span class='zh'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。核心在于新机制可能改变团队的协作、历史验证和开发扩展的方式。对开发者来说，这预示着工具链与信任体系的变革。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://9to5mac.com/2026/03/26/apple-discontinues-the-mac-pro/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple discontinues the Mac Pro</span><span class='zh'>苹果停产 Mac Pro</span></a><span class="dom">9to5mac.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple has ended the Mac Pro line, signaling a shift away from modular Xeon-based workstations toward higher-density consumer-grade hardware or external compute options.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布停产 Mac Pro，这标志着其工作站路线向高密度消费级硬件或外部计算方案的转变。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple has ended the Mac Pro line, signaling a shift away from modular Xeon-based workstations toward higher-density consumer-grade hardware or external compute options. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Le Monde investigation shows real-time tracking of France&#x27;s aircraft carrier via fitness app data, revealing how public location sharing can leak sensitive security information.</span><span class='zh'>勒蒙德的调查显示，公开的健身应用数据可能让法国航母的位置在实时暴露，暴露了公共数据对国家安全的潜在威胁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Le Monde investigation shows real-time tracking of France&#x27;s aircraft carrier via fitness app data, revealing how public location sharing can leak sensitive security information.</span><span class='zh'>勒蒙德的调查显示，公开的健身应用数据可能让法国航母的位置在实时暴露，暴露了公共数据对国家安全的潜在威胁。此事凸显了个人位置信息在缺乏隐私保护的情况下对安全的影响，促使对数据共享进行审视。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: after reclaiming control of Video.js, the team rebuilt the project to a leaner 12% of its former size, maintaining compatibility while shaving bandwidth and load time.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：重新掌控 Video.js 后，团队将其重写，使体积仅为原来的 12%，并保持向后兼容，同时减小带宽和加载时间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: after reclaiming control of Video.js, the team rebuilt the project to a leaner 12% of its former size, maintaining compatibility while shaving bandwidth and load time. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The e Foundation positions /e/OS as a fully de-Googled mobile ecosystem with privacy-first apps and open-source approaches, aiming to rival mainstream platforms while retaining broad app compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>/e/OS 被描述为一个完整的去谷歌化移动生态，强调隐私优先的应用与开源替代，力求在保持应用兼容的同时挑战主流平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The e Foundation positions /e/OS as a fully de-Googled mobile ecosystem with privacy-first apps and open-source approaches, aiming to rival mainstream platforms while retaining broad app compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>/e/OS 被描述为一个完整的去谷歌化移动生态，强调隐私优先的应用与开源替代，力求在保持应用兼容的同时挑战主流平台。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://xn--gckvb8fzb.com/hold-on-to-your-hardware/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hold on to Your Hardware</span><span class='zh'>守住你的硬件</span></a><span class="dom">xn--gckvb8fzb.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A short essay argues for extending hardware lifespans through repairable design, modular upgrades, and stronger anti-obsolescence norms.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张通过可维修设计、模块化升级和更强的反废弃规范来延长硬件寿命，警示随电子产品推陈出新导致的电子废弃物增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A short essay argues for extending hardware lifespans through repairable design, modular upgrades, and stronger anti-obsolescence norms. It warns that consumer electronics waste grows as devices become harder to repair, urging both makers and buyers to rethink responsibility.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张通过可维修设计、模块化升级和更强的反废弃规范来延长硬件寿命，警示随电子产品推陈出新导致的电子废弃物增加。作者呼吁厂商与用户共同重塑责任感，以减缓浪费并提升可持续性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A whistleblower alleges a former DOGE member copied Social Security data and brought it to a new job. The claim highlights insider risk and weak data-handling practices, underscoring the need for stronger access controls, monitoring, and data exfiltration safeguards in organizations handling sensitive numbers.</span><span class='zh'>一名举报者声称，前 DOGE 成员将社会安全号码数据带到新工作岗位。此事暴露了内部威胁与数据处理的薄弱环节，强调在处理敏感信息的组织中需加强访问控制、审计与数据外泄防护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://grith.ai/blog/clinejection-when-your-ai-tool-installs-another" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A GitHub Issue Title Compromised 4k Developer Machines</span><span class='zh'>一个 GitHub 问题标题被劫持，影响约4千台开发者机器</span></a><span class="dom">grith.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A blog suggests that a crafted GitHub issue title could trigger an injection in an AI-assisted workflow, potentially compromising thousands of developer machines.</span><span class='zh'>一篇博文指出，通过设计的 GitHub issue 标题可能在 AI 助手工作流中触发命令注入，进而影响数千名开发者的机器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A blog suggests that a crafted GitHub issue title could trigger an injection in an AI-assisted workflow, potentially compromising thousands of developer machines. The post underscores the need for input sanitization, strict tool boundaries, and provenance verification when AI is integrated with code hosting.</span><span class='zh'>一篇博文指出，通过设计的 GitHub issue 标题可能在 AI 助手工作流中触发命令注入，进而影响数千名开发者的机器。此事强调在将 AI 与代码托管服务结合使用时需加强输入过滤、边界控制和来源可信度的验证。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://hacks.mozilla.org/2026/02/making-webassembly-a-first-class-language-on-the-web/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=hacks.mozilla.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>hacks.mozilla.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hacks.mozilla.org/2026/02/making-webassembly-a-first-class-language-on-the-web/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Making WebAssembly a first-class language on the Web</span><span class='zh'>让 WebAssembly 成为 Web 上的一等语言</span></a><span class="dom">hacks.mozilla.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Efforts to make WebAssembly a first-class language on the Web focus on richer runtime features—like GC, component model, interface types, and better debugging—to let high-level languages interoperate and be authored for the browser without forcing JavaScript as the only host.</span><span class='zh'>将 WebAssembly 打造成 Web 上的一等语言需要补齐运行时功能（如垃圾回收、组件模型、接口类型和更好的调试支持），以便高阶语言能无缝互操作并原生面向浏览器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Efforts to make WebAssembly a first-class language on the Web focus on richer runtime features—like GC, component model, interface types, and better debugging—to let high-level languages interoperate and be authored for the browser without forcing JavaScript as the only host. If successful, this work would broaden language choice for web apps, improve performance portability, and unlock tooling and deployment patterns closer to native languages.</span><span class='zh'>将 WebAssembly 打造成 Web 上的一等语言需要补齐运行时功能（如垃圾回收、组件模型、接口类型和更好的调试支持），以便高阶语言能无缝互操作并原生面向浏览器。若能实现，这将扩展网页的语言选择、提升性能可移植性，并带来更接近本地开发的工具链和部署方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A sufficiently detailed spec is code</span><span class='zh'>足够详细的规格就是代码</span></a><span class="dom">haskellforall.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Detailed specifications can serve as concrete, testable artifacts that drive implementation without separate code.</span><span class='zh'>足够详尽的规格本身就能成为可执行的产物，推动实现、测试与仿真，模糊了文档与代码的界线。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Detailed specifications can serve as concrete, testable artifacts that drive implementation without separate code. When specs are precise, teams can simulate behavior and verify outcomes directly from the docs.</span><span class='zh'>足够详尽的规格本身就能成为可执行的产物，推动实现、测试与仿真，模糊了文档与代码的界线。把规范当作代码来管理，可以用自动化校验与模拟来验证行为。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jai.scs.stanford.edu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Go hard on agents, not on your filesystem</span><span class='zh'>把精力放在代理上，而不是文件系统</span></a><span class="dom">jai.scs.stanford.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This view argues that you should design around lightweight agents that orchestrate tasks and data flows rather than stuffing complexity into the filesystem.</span><span class='zh'>该观点主张围绕轻量代理来编排任务和数据流，而不是把复杂性堆在文件系统上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This view argues that you should design around lightweight agents that orchestrate tasks and data flows rather than stuffing complexity into the filesystem. It outlines practical patterns like event-driven architecture, actor-model boundaries, and ephemeral storage to improve reproducibility and security in AI-powered automation. Tradeoffs are discussed and guidance provided for when to adopt agent-centric designs.</span><span class='zh'>该观点主张围绕轻量代理来编排任务和数据流，而不是把复杂性堆在文件系统上。文中给出事件驱动架构、演员模型边界、瞬态存储等实用模式，以提升可重复性、安全性和在 AI 自动化中的可组合性。还讨论了权衡以及在何种场景应采用以代理为中心的设计。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Cornell piece suggests that teams heavy on buzzwords like synergizing paradigms may underperform, emphasizing concrete problem-solving over abstract rhetoric.</span><span class='zh'>Cornell 的研究称，过分依赖“协同范式”等空洞术语的员工可能业绩不佳，强调解决问题的具体性比空谈更重要。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Cornell piece suggests that teams heavy on buzzwords like synergizing paradigms may underperform, emphasizing concrete problem-solving over abstract rhetoric. The message resonates in tech culture where clear outcomes beat grand metaphors.</span><span class='zh'>Cornell 的研究称，过分依赖“协同范式”等空洞术语的员工可能业绩不佳，强调解决问题的具体性比空谈更重要。这一观点在科技团队中尤为相关。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A deep dive into the optimization practices behind RollerCoaster Tycoon, the piece argues that the game&#x27;s engine exemplifies rigorous, scalable performance engineering.</span><span class='zh'>对 RollerCoaster Tycoon 的底层优化实践进行深入解读，认为游戏引擎体现了严格且可扩展的性能工程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A deep dive into the optimization practices behind RollerCoaster Tycoon, the piece argues that the game&#x27;s engine exemplifies rigorous, scalable performance engineering. It reveals the tradeoffs between simulation fidelity and render performance, offering lessons for real-time systems and game AI.</span><span class='zh'>对 RollerCoaster Tycoon 的底层优化实践进行深入解读，认为游戏引擎体现了严格且可扩展的性能工程。揭示仿真精度与渲染性能之间的权衡，为实时系统和游戏 AI 提供可借鉴的经验。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A journalist is fired after allegations that quotes attributed to AI were fabricated.</span><span class='zh'>因涉及 AI 捏造引述的争议，记者被解雇。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://stevekrouse.com/precision" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reports of code&#x27;s death are greatly exaggerated</span><span class='zh'>关于代码死亡的传闻被大大夸大</span></a><span class="dom">stevekrouse.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen.</span><span class='zh'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen. The piece argues that precise, maintainable code, rigorous testing, and clear design remain essential as automation shifts routine work. In practice, expect a continued emphasis on tooling that preserves correctness and developer control.</span><span class='zh'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。高质量、可维护的代码、严谨的测试和明确的设计仍然是关键，因为自动化会改变工作方式但需要人类把控。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47296547" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: How to be alone?</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：如何学会独处？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://help.obsidian.md/sync/headless" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Obsidian Sync now has a headless client</span><span class='zh'>Obsidian Sync 现已推出无头客户端</span></a><span class="dom">help.obsidian.md</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://channelsurfer.tv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Channel Surfer – Watch YouTube like it’s cable TV</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Channel Surfer — 像看有线电视一样观看 YouTube</span></a><span class="dom">channelsurfer.tv</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Channel Surfer offers a TV-like, channel-based browsing UX for YouTube, making it feel like flipping through channels rather than scrolling feeds.</span><span class='zh'>Channel Surfer 提供了类似有线电视的频道式浏览 UI，让 YouTube 的观看体验像在切换频道，而非刷订阅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Channel Surfer offers a TV-like, channel-based browsing UX for YouTube, making it feel like flipping through channels rather than scrolling feeds. It emphasizes curated discovery and continuous viewing without the usual YouTube UI clutter. The concept highlights how alternative interfaces can change how users consume long-form video on the web.</span><span class='zh'>Channel Surfer 提供了类似有线电视的频道式浏览 UI，让 YouTube 的观看体验像在切换频道，而非刷订阅。通过更便捷的聚合与连续播放，它去除了常规界面的干扰，强调了对长视频的发现与连贯观看。这个想法展示了替代界面如何改变网络视频的消费方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Using Wave Function Collapse to generate hex maps, this guide walks through tile constraints and edge matching for procedural world building.</span><span class='zh'>本文展示如何将波函数坍缩算法应用于六边形网格的程序化地图生成，讲解瓷砖约束与边界匹配的要点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Using Wave Function Collapse to generate hex maps, this guide walks through tile constraints and edge matching for procedural world building. The approach offers a reusable pattern for designers seeking deterministic, tweakable map generation.</span><span class='zh'>本文展示如何将波函数坍缩算法应用于六边形网格的程序化地图生成，讲解瓷砖约束与边界匹配的要点。内容还讨论了性能考量及将 WFC 集成到游戏工具链的实用技巧。此方法为追求确定性、可调地图的设计者提供了可复用的模板。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LLM Architecture Gallery presents a visual catalog of common and emerging LLM architectures, from vanilla transformer stacks to retrieval-augmented and modular designs.</span><span class='zh'>LLM 架构画廊以可视化形式整理了常见与新兴的模型架构，从基础的 Transformer 堆栈到检索增强和模块化设计，帮助工程师对比在延迟、内存与精度等维度的权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LLM Architecture Gallery presents a visual catalog of common and emerging LLM architectures, from vanilla transformer stacks to retrieval-augmented and modular designs. It helps engineers compare trade-offs in latency, memory, and accuracy. Takeaway: architecture choices shape capabilities and cost, and a visual guide helps map the design space.</span><span class='zh'>LLM 架构画廊以可视化形式整理了常见与新兴的模型架构，从基础的 Transformer 堆栈到检索增强和模块化设计，帮助工程师对比在延迟、内存与精度等维度的权衡。要点：架构选择决定能力和成本，直观的对比有助于把握设计空间。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bsky.app/profile/tuta.com/post/3mhxkfowv322c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>European Parliament decided that Chat Control 1.0 must stop</span><span class='zh'>欧洲议会决定停止&#x27;聊天监控1.0&#x27;</span></a><span class="dom">bsky.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>European Parliament voted to halt Chat Control 1.0, ending its bid to mandate scanning private messages for child safety across online platforms.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲议会投票决定终止&#x27;聊天监控1.0&#x27;的推进，放弃在私密通讯中进行大规模扫描的立法路径。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>European Parliament voted to halt Chat Control 1.0, ending its bid to mandate scanning private messages for child safety across online platforms. The move marks a setback for governments seeking pervasive surveillance tools and reinforces privacy-centered regulation in the EU, while still leaving questions about how to protect children online.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲议会投票决定终止&#x27;聊天监控1.0&#x27;的推进，放弃在私密通讯中进行大规模扫描的立法路径。此举缓解了对数字隐私的担忧，但也引发关于儿童保护与执法能力的持续讨论，未来欧盟在该议题上的路径仍不确定。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://writings.hongminhee.org/2026/03/legal-vs-legitimate/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is legal the same as legitimate: AI reimplementation and the erosion of copyleft</span><span class='zh'>法律与正当性：AI 重现与 Copyleft 的侵蚀</span></a><span class="dom">writings.hongminhee.org</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A thoughtful look at the indie or small web beyond big platforms, highlighting personal sites, RSS, and distributed hosting. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Ninth Circuit says email notices can update terms of service, and continued use after notice can imply consent.</span><span class='zh'>第九巡回法院裁定通过电子邮件通知更新服务条款是有效的，用户在通知后继续使用可构成默示同意。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Ninth Circuit says email notices can update terms of service, and continued use after notice can imply consent. This underscores how electronic notices shape online contracts and the legal weight of user conduct.</span><span class='zh'>第九巡回法院裁定通过电子邮件通知更新服务条款是有效的，用户在通知后继续使用可构成默示同意。这凸显了电子通知在在线合约中的法律效力，以及用户行为所承载的同意。</span></p>
