<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-copilot/for-individuals/termsofuse" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft: Copilot is for entertainment purposes only</span><span class='zh'>微软：Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途</span></a><span class="dom">microsoft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations.</span><span class='zh'>微软在使用条款中明确表示 Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途，界定了其使用范围与局限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations. This stance raises questions about relying on AI for critical coding or decision-making and highlights the need for clear risk disclosures.</span><span class='zh'>微软在使用条款中明确表示 Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途，界定了其使用范围与局限。此说法引发了人们对将其用于关键编码或决策场景的可依赖性以及需披露的风险信息的关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ogirardot.writizzy.com/p/good-software-knows-when-to-stop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Good software knows when to stop</span><span class='zh'>好软件知道何时该停下来</span></a><span class="dom">ogirardot.writizzy.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A piece argues for restraint in software, warning against feature creep and unnecessary complexity.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张软件开发需要克制，反对无休止的功能堆叠与复杂性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A piece argues for restraint in software, warning against feature creep and unnecessary complexity. Focus on reliability, security, and maintainability—letting tools do a few things well rather than many mediocre ones.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张软件开发需要克制，反对无休止的功能堆叠与复杂性。强调以可靠性、安保和可维护性为核心，让工具专注做好几件事，而不是堆砌大量功能。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>535</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47261561' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>269</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47464818" data-id="47464818" data-tags="Privacy|Legal|AI" data-score="533" data-comments="149" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47464818" data-time="1774078216">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/03/blocking-internet-archive-wont-stop-ai-it-will-erase-webs-historical-record" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blocking Internet Archive Won&#x27;t Stop AI, but Will Erase Web&#x27;s Historical Record</span><span class='zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会阻止 AI，但会抹去网络的历史记录</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Blocking Internet Archive won&#x27;t curb AI development, and models will still train on data from other sources.</span><span class='zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会遏制 AI 发展，模型仍会从其他来源获取数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Blocking Internet Archive won&#x27;t curb AI development, and models will still train on data from other sources. It would erase a vital historical snapshot of the web, harming researchers, journalists, and accountability. Smarter data-use policies are preferable to outright blackouts.</span><span class='zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会遏制 AI 发展，模型仍会从其他来源获取数据。这会抹去网络的宝贵历史快照，损害研究、报道和问责能力。应采用更聪明的数据使用政策，而不是全面封锁。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://research.google/blog/turboquant-redefining-ai-efficiency-with-extreme-compression/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TurboQuant: Redefining AI efficiency with extreme compression</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant：以极端压缩重塑AI效率</span></a><span class="dom">research.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TurboQuant introduces extreme compression techniques to shrink AI models and cut compute needs while preserving accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant 引入极端压缩技术，在保持准确度的同时显著降低模型规模和计算需求，有望降低大规模部署成本并推动边缘设备或端侧推理的普及。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TurboQuant introduces extreme compression techniques to shrink AI models and cut compute needs while preserving accuracy. This approach tests the boundaries of model distillation and quantization, potentially slashing deployment costs and enabling more edge and on-device inference. Trade-offs remain: training compatibility and potential accuracy quirks in edge cases.</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant 引入极端压缩技术，在保持准确度的同时显著降低模型规模和计算需求，有望降低大规模部署成本并推动边缘设备或端侧推理的普及。该方法可能带来训练兼容性与在极端情形下的精度波动等权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mlu-explain.github.io/decision-tree/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decision trees – the unreasonable power of nested decision rules</span><span class='zh'>决策树：嵌套决策规则的强大力量</span></a><span class="dom">mlu-explain.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nested decision rules let a decision tree encode nuanced logic with transparent paths, but depth can make them unwieldy.</span><span class='zh'>嵌套决策规则使决策树能够以透明的路径表达细致的逻辑，但深度增加会使其变得难以维护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nested decision rules let a decision tree encode nuanced logic with transparent paths, but depth can make them unwieldy. With careful structure and pruning, they remain a practical tool for governable, explainable rules even as data-driven models rise.</span><span class='zh'>嵌套决策规则使决策树能够以透明的路径表达细致的逻辑，但深度增加会使其变得难以维护。通过合适的结构和剪枝，它仍然是可解释性强、可治理的规则工具，即便在数据驱动模型盛行时也有用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta renews its commitment to jemalloc, the memory allocator powering its data infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 再次承诺继续优化 jemalloc，这一为数据基础设施提供分配内存的组件在大规模系统中扮演关键角色。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta renews its commitment to jemalloc, the memory allocator powering its data infrastructure. The move signals continued focus on performance, latency, and memory efficiency at scale, with potential upstream contributions benefiting other projects that rely on jemalloc.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 再次承诺继续优化 jemalloc，这一为数据基础设施提供分配内存的组件在大规模系统中扮演关键角色。此举表明 Meta 将持续提升性能与内存效率，并可能向 jemalloc 的开源社区带来贡献，惠及依赖该分配器的其他项目。</span></p>

The post underscores how licensing choices affect community adoption and collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>探讨关于对一个项目进行再授权的许可权问题，认为作者在未获得同意的情况下不能单方面改变授权，或指出再授权权利存在争议。文章强调许可选择会影响社区采纳与协作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mandel-macaque/memento" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If AI writes code, should the session be part of the commit?</span><span class='zh'>如果 AI 写代码，会话应成为提交的一部分吗？</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>If AI-assisted sessions influence code changes, teams may consider embedding session provenance in commits or metadata for traceability.</span><span class='zh'>若 AI 辅助的编辑会影响代码变更，团队可考虑在提交中嵌入会话出处或元数据以实现可追溯性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>If AI-assisted sessions influence code changes, teams may consider embedding session provenance in commits or metadata for traceability. This raises questions about reproducibility, review, and auditing of AI-influenced work.</span><span class='zh'>若 AI 辅助的编辑会影响代码变更，团队可考虑在提交中嵌入会话出处或元数据以实现可追溯性。这引发关于可复现性、审查与对 AI 影响的审计等问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/itigges22/ATLAS" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>$500 GPU outperforms Claude Sonnet on coding benchmarks</span><span class='zh'>500 美元 GPU 在编码基准测试中胜过 Claude Sonnet</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A $500 GPU reportedly outperforms Claude Sonnet on coding benchmarks, signaling that consumer hardware can rival dedicated AI accelerators for certain tasks.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，价值 500 美元的显卡在编码基准测试中胜过 Claude Sonnet，表明部分任务上普通硬件也能与专用 AI 加速器对抗。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A $500 GPU reportedly outperforms Claude Sonnet on coding benchmarks, signaling that consumer hardware can rival dedicated AI accelerators for certain tasks. This shifts cost considerations for AI research and development.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，价值 500 美元的显卡在编码基准测试中胜过 Claude Sonnet，表明部分任务上普通硬件也能与专用 AI 加速器对抗。这将改变 AI 研究与开发的成本考量。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed a lawsuit against OpenCode, signaling a new legal tussle in the AI ecosystem. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini now supports native video embedding, enabling richer media integration in AI workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 现已原生支持嵌入视频，方便在 AI 流程中整合多媒体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini now supports native video embedding, enabling richer media integration in AI workflows. The author built a fast video search system leveraging embeddings to index video frames and transcripts, achieving sub-second lookup. This demonstrates how client-side media capabilities can enable near-instant content discovery.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 现已原生支持嵌入视频，方便在 AI 流程中整合多媒体。作者基于嵌入向量构建了视频搜索系统，对视频帧与文字转写进行索引，实现亚秒级检索，展示了前端对多媒体内容进行近乎即时检索的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.com/IsolatedOctopi/nvidia_greenboost" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia greenboost: transparently extend GPU VRAM using system RAM/NVMe</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA GreenBoost：透明扩展显存，利用系统内存/ NVMe</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GreenBoost proposes using host RAM and NVMe as a transparent extension of GPU VRAM, enabling larger-than-GPU memory workloads with potential performance costs.</span><span class='zh'>GreenBoost 提议将主机内存与 NVMe 作为显存的透明扩展，使在显存受限时也能处理更大规模的工作负载，代价是潜在的性能开销和延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GreenBoost proposes using host RAM and NVMe as a transparent extension of GPU VRAM, enabling larger-than-GPU memory workloads with potential performance costs. The project highlights memory management tradeoffs that could influence future GPU design.</span><span class='zh'>GreenBoost 提议将主机内存与 NVMe 作为显存的透明扩展，使在显存受限时也能处理更大规模的工作负载，代价是潜在的性能开销和延迟。该思路若落地，可能影响未来 GPU 的内存管理策略和驱动设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/novatic14/MANPADS-System-Launcher-and-Rocket" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>$96 3D-printed rocket that recalculates its mid-air trajectory using a $5 sensor</span><span class='zh'>96 美元的 3D 打印火箭利用 5 美元传感器在空中重新计算轨迹</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A $96 3D-printed rocket uses a $5 sensor to recalculate its trajectory mid-flight, showcasing how affordable components enable advanced control experiments.</span><span class='zh'>一个价值 96 美元的 3D 打印火箭，利用 5 美元传感器在飞行中重新计算轨迹，展示了低成本部件如何支持高端控制实验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A $96 3D-printed rocket uses a $5 sensor to recalculate its trajectory mid-flight, showcasing how affordable components enable advanced control experiments. It highlights both impressive tinkering and the safety/regulatory gaps between hobbyist builds and regulated rocketry.</span><span class='zh'>一个价值 96 美元的 3D 打印火箭，利用 5 美元传感器在飞行中重新计算轨迹，展示了低成本部件如何支持高端控制实验。此举凸显了爱好者探索的惊人性，同时也暴露了从业余试验到受监管火箭活动之间的安全与法规差距。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/neovim/neovim/releases/tag/v0.12.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neovim 0.12.0</span><span class='zh'>Neovim 0.12.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neovim 0.12.0 marks a significant release with enhanced performance and user experience, continuing the Lua-driven configuration and richer plugin ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>Neovim 0.12.0 作为一次重要版本更新，提升了性能和用户体验，延续 Lua 驱动的配置与更丰富的插件生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neovim 0.12.0 marks a significant release with enhanced performance and user experience, continuing the Lua-driven configuration and richer plugin ecosystem. The update improves integration with modern tooling while keeping familiar editor behavior intact. Developers benefit from smoother workflows and extensibility.</span><span class='zh'>Neovim 0.12.0 作为一次重要版本更新，提升了性能和用户体验，延续 Lua 驱动的配置与更丰富的插件生态。改动改善与现代工具链的集成，同时保持编辑器的核心行为，提升开发者的工作流与扩展能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/danveloper/flash-moe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE: Running a 397B Parameter Model on a Laptop</span><span class='zh'>Flash-MoE：在笔记本上运行397B参数模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware.</span><span class='zh'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware. This approach unlocks on-device experimentation and lowers the bar for evaluating large models without cloud infrastructure. Expect more on-device AI prototyping and edge-friendly deployments.</span><span class='zh'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。此举降低了评估大模型对云资源的依赖，促进本地化实验与边缘部署的发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/adamamyl/81b78eced40feae50eae7c4f3bec1f5a" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>macOS 26 breaks custom DNS settings including .internal</span><span class='zh'>macOS 26 破坏了自定义 DNS 设置，包括 .internal</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>macOS 26 changes break the ability to override DNS settings, including .internal domains, impacting local development and privacy.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 26 版本取消了部分自定义 DNS 设置（包括 .internal），对本地开发与隐私可能造成影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>macOS 26 changes break the ability to override DNS settings, including .internal domains, impacting local development and privacy. The post outlines workarounds and the scope of impact.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 26 版本取消了部分自定义 DNS 设置（包括 .internal），对本地开发与隐私可能造成影响。博主给出可能的工作绕过方法并讨论影响范围。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia NemoClaw</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA 发布 NemoClaw</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA releases NemoClaw on GitHub as part of the NeMo AI tooling ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA 在 GitHub 上推出 NemoClaw，作为 NeMo 人工智能工具生态的一部分。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA releases NemoClaw on GitHub as part of the NeMo AI tooling ecosystem. The project appears to be an experimental utility designed to simplify building or managing AI models, hinting at Nvidia&#x27;s ongoing push to modularize developer tooling for AI workloads. As with many in-progress projects, it signals where Nvidia is investing next in developer UX around AI.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA 在 GitHub 上推出 NemoClaw，作为 NeMo 人工智能工具生态的一部分。该项目似乎是一个实验性实用工具，旨在简化构建或管理 AI 模型，体现了 Nvidia 持续推动 AI 开发工具模块化的方向。对于这类尚在探索阶段的项目，它暗示了 Nvidia 在 AI 开发体验方面的下一步投资重点。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cowork unexpectedly creates a 10GB VM bundle on macOS, raising concerns about disk usage and UX clarity for AI-enabled developer tools.</span><span class='zh'>Cowork 功能在 macOS 上意外生成 10GB 的虚拟机捆绑包，影响磁盘空间与性能，引发对开发工具 UX 和资源管理的关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/BitNet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>BitNet: Inference framework for 1-bit LLMs</span><span class='zh'>BitNet：用于1位LLM推断的框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BitNet is an inference framework for 1-bit LLMs, enabling smaller memory footprints and lower compute requirements.</span><span class='zh'>BitNet 是一个用于 1 位 LLM 推断的框架，使内存占用和计算需求显著降低。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BitNet is an inference framework for 1-bit LLMs, enabling smaller memory footprints and lower compute requirements. By quantizing to 1-bit representations, it demonstrates how large language models can run more efficiently, albeit with trade-offs in accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>BitNet 是一个用于 1 位 LLM 推断的框架，使内存占用和计算需求显著降低。通过对权重与激活进行 1 位量化，它展示了在性能受限的场景中运行大语言模型的可行性，同时也存在准确度的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chenglou/pretext" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pretext: TypeScript library for multiline text measurement and layout</span><span class='zh'>Pretext：用于多行文本测量与布局的 TypeScript 库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pretext provides a TypeScript library for accurate multiline text measurement and layout.</span><span class='zh'>Pretext 提供一个用于多行文本测量与布局的 TypeScript 库，帮助在不同字体与字号下实现可预期的换行与高度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pretext provides a TypeScript library for accurate multiline text measurement and layout. It helps developers render dynamic text with predictable wrapping and height, reducing layout bugs across fonts and sizes. This can be especially valuable for canvas or SVG-based UI work.</span><span class='zh'>Pretext 提供一个用于多行文本测量与布局的 TypeScript 库，帮助在不同字体与字号下实现可预期的换行与高度。对于需要精确文本渲染的场景（如画布或 SVG UI）特别有用，减少布局错误。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/datasets/open-index/hacker-news" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hacker News archive (47M+ items, 11.6GB) as Parquet, updated every 5m</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：以 Parquet 存储的 Hacker News 归档（4700万+ 条，11.6GB），每5分钟更新</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open dataset project publishing a massive Hacker News archive in Parquet format, enabling fast analytics and ML workflows.</span><span class='zh'>该开源数据集项目将 Hacker News 的庞大归档以 Parquet 格式发布，便于快速分析与机器学习工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open dataset project publishing a massive Hacker News archive in Parquet format, enabling fast analytics and ML workflows. Updated every five minutes, it offers near-real-time access to historical data for researchers and enthusiasts. It&#x27;s a practical data infrastructure example for the HN ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>该开源数据集项目将 Hacker News 的庞大归档以 Parquet 格式发布，便于快速分析与机器学习工作流。每五分钟更新一次，接近实时地提供历史数据，适合研究者与爱好者使用。这是面向 HN 生态的数据基础设施的一个实际案例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/62026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UUID package coming to Go standard library</span><span class='zh'>Go 标准库将新增 UUID 包</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Go is adding a standard UUID package, standardizing globally unique identifiers across projects.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将引入标准库中的 UUID 包，统一跨项目的全局唯一标识符用法，减少依赖差异并提升互操作性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Go is adding a standard UUID package, standardizing globally unique identifiers across projects. This reduces dependency drift and helps ensure consistent encoding, parsing, and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将引入标准库中的 UUID 包，统一跨项目的全局唯一标识符用法，减少依赖差异并提升互操作性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Hammerspoon/hammerspoon" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hammerspoon</span><span class='zh'>Hammerspoon：macOS 自动化工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hammerspoon remains a mature macOS automation engine, letting power users script system actions with Lua and connect to native apps for deep customization and productivity gains.</span><span class='zh'>Hammerspoon 仍是成熟的 macOS 自动化工具，用户可以用 Lua 脚本控制系统并与本地应用深度整合，提升生产力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hammerspoon remains a mature macOS automation engine, letting power users script system actions with Lua and connect to native apps for deep customization and productivity gains.</span><span class='zh'>Hammerspoon 仍是成熟的 macOS 自动化工具，用户可以用 Lua 脚本控制系统并与本地应用深度整合，提升生产力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ad-si/Woxi" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Woxi: Wolfram Mathematica Reimplementation in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Woxi：用 Rust 重实现 Wolfram Mathematica</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Woxi is a Rust-based reimplementation of Wolfram Mathematica, aiming to deliver high-performance symbolic computation.</span><span class='zh'>Woxi 用 Rust 重实现 Wolfram Mathematica，目标是提供高性能的符号计算工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Woxi is a Rust-based reimplementation of Wolfram Mathematica, aiming to deliver high-performance symbolic computation. It exemplifies efforts to democratize advanced math tooling while highlighting the challenges of reimplementing a mature system.</span><span class='zh'>Woxi 用 Rust 重实现 Wolfram Mathematica，目标是提供高性能的符号计算工具。该项目展示了让高端数学工具更易得的努力，同时也揭示了重实现一个成熟系统的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/resumex/doom-over-dns" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DOOM Over DNS</span><span class='zh'>DNS 上的 DOOM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Doom Over DNS is a proof-of-concept that streams data or a game over DNS channels, illustrating DNS&#x27;s potential as a transport medium and its security implications.</span><span class='zh'>DOOM Over DNS 是一个展示通过 DNS 通道传输数据或游戏的概念验证，揭示 DNS 作为传输媒介的潜力及其安全含义。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Doom Over DNS is a proof-of-concept that streams data or a game over DNS channels, illustrating DNS&#x27;s potential as a transport medium and its security implications. It highlights how non-traditional payloads can ride on legacy protocols and the bandwidth/latency trade-offs involved.</span><span class='zh'>DOOM Over DNS 是一个展示通过 DNS 通道传输数据或游戏的概念验证，揭示 DNS 作为传输媒介的潜力及其安全含义。它强调传统协议也能承载非传统载荷，以及相关的带宽与延迟权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/electrikmilk/cherri" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cherri – programming language that compiles to an Apple Shortuct</span><span class='zh'>Cherri：一门可编译为苹果快捷指令的编程语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cherri is a programming language that compiles to Apple Shortcuts, letting developers write automation logic in a higher-level, typed syntax and then produce the iOS automation graph.</span><span class='zh'>Cherri 是一门可编译为 Apple Shortcuts 的编程语言，允许开发者用更高层、带类型的语法编写自动化逻辑，再生成 iOS 的 Shortcuts 工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cherri is a programming language that compiles to Apple Shortcuts, letting developers write automation logic in a higher-level, typed syntax and then produce the iOS automation graph. It lowers the barrier to complex automations beyond the native Shortcuts UI, while still targeting the same automation platform.</span><span class='zh'>Cherri 是一门可编译为 Apple Shortcuts 的编程语言，允许开发者用更高层、带类型的语法编写自动化逻辑，再生成 iOS 的 Shortcuts 工作流。它让实现复杂自动化的门槛低于原生 Shortcuts 界面，同时仍然面向同一自动化平台。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/austin-weeks/miasma" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Miasma: A tool to trap AI web scrapers in an endless poison pit</span><span class='zh'>Miasma：一款用来把 AI 网页爬虫困在无尽的“毒坑”中的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Miasma is a defensive tool that traps AI web scrapers by injecting decoy data and routing tricks to exhaust their resources.</span><span class='zh'>Miasma 提供一种防御性手段，通过注入诱饵数据和路由技巧让 AI 爬虫耗费资源以达到阻止抓取的目的。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Miasma is a defensive tool that traps AI web scrapers by injecting decoy data and routing tricks to exhaust their resources. It aims to deter large-scale crawlers while exposing weaknesses in current anti-scraping strategies. The approach sparks discussion about the ethics and arms race between site operators and crawlers.</span><span class='zh'>Miasma 提供一种防御性手段，通过注入诱饵数据和路由技巧让 AI 爬虫耗费资源以达到阻止抓取的目的。它旨在抑制大规模爬虫的行为，同时暴露现有反爬策略的薄弱点，引发关于网络防御伦理与爬虫对抗的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cockpit-project/cockpit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cockpit is a web-based graphical interface for servers</span><span class='zh'>Cockpit：一个基于网页的服务器图形界面</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cockpit provides a browser-based UI to monitor and manage Linux servers, integrating with system services, containers, and logs to simplify remote administration beyond SSH-only workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Cockpit 提供基于浏览器的服务器管理界面，便于远程监控和配置 Linux 服务器，覆盖系统服务、容器与日志等功能，降低了对 SSH 作为唯一入口的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cockpit provides a browser-based UI to monitor and manage Linux servers, integrating with system services, containers, and logs to simplify remote administration beyond SSH-only workflows. Its modular, open-source approach makes it appealing for ops teams seeking a lightweight control plane.</span><span class='zh'>Cockpit 提供基于浏览器的服务器管理界面，便于远程监控和配置 Linux 服务器，覆盖系统服务、容器与日志等功能，降低了对 SSH 作为唯一入口的依赖。其模块化、开源特性使运维团队更容易搭建轻量级的控制平面。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/astral-sh/uv/pull/17643" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Warn about PyPy being unmaintained</span><span class='zh'>警告：PyPy 不再维护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Discussion about flagging PyPy as unmaintained, highlighting concerns about compatibility, security, and the Python ecosystem&#x27;s reliance on alternative runtimes.</span><span class='zh'>关于对 PyPy 不再维护的标注，突显兼容性、安全性与 Python 生态对替代运行时的依赖等议题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Discussion about flagging PyPy as unmaintained, highlighting concerns about compatibility, security, and the Python ecosystem&#x27;s reliance on alternative runtimes.</span><span class='zh'>关于对 PyPy 不再维护的标注，突显兼容性、安全性与 Python 生态对替代运行时的依赖等议题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/J-x-Z/cocoa-way" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cocoa-Way – Native macOS Wayland compositor for running Linux apps seamlessly</span><span class='zh'>Cocoa-Way：原生 macOS Wayland 合成器，实现 Linux 应用无缝运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cocoa-Way aims to be a native macOS Wayland compositor to run Linux apps with minimal friction, integrating with Cocoa for window management and rendering.</span><span class='zh'>Cocoa-Way 旨在成为原生 macOS 的 Wayland 合成器，以最小化摩擦地在 macOS 上运行 Linux 应用，并与 Cocoa 进行窗口管理和渲染整合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cocoa-Way aims to be a native macOS Wayland compositor to run Linux apps with minimal friction, integrating with Cocoa for window management and rendering. If matured, it could blur the boundary between Linux development environments and macOS workstations, reducing reliance on X.org or VM-based workarounds.</span><span class='zh'>Cocoa-Way 旨在成为原生 macOS 的 Wayland 合成器，以最小化摩擦地在 macOS 上运行 Linux 应用，并与 Cocoa 进行窗口管理和渲染整合。若成熟，将模糊 Linux 开发环境与 macOS 工作站之间的边界，减少对 Xorg 或虚拟机的依赖。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/03/28/top_stories_03282026'><span class='en'>Mar 28</span><span class='zh'>3月28日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>309</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553185' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>104</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-47583045" data-id="47583045" data-tags="AI|Data|Code" data-score="303" data-comments="105" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47583045" data-time="1774934519">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-research/timesfm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s 200M-parameter time-series foundation model with 16k context</span><span class='zh'>谷歌发布 200M 参数的时序基础模型，具 16k 上下文容量</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google Research unveils TimesFM, a 200M-parameter time-series foundation model with a 16k context window.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌研究院发布 TimesFM，一个200M参数的时序基础模型，具备16k上下文窗口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google Research unveils TimesFM, a 200M-parameter time-series foundation model with a 16k context window. The model demonstrates that compact architectures can achieve long-range forecasting, unlocking more capable edge and on-device time-series analytics with lower compute budgets.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌研究院发布 TimesFM，一个200M参数的时序基础模型，具备16k上下文窗口。该模型表明紧凑架构也能实现长距离预测，推动边缘和本地时序分析在更低算力预算下实现。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/adammiribyan/zeroboot" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Sub-millisecond VM sandboxes using CoW memory forking</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 使用 CoW 内存分叉实现亚毫秒级 VM 沙箱</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates sub-millisecond VM sandboxes built with Copy-On-Write memory forking.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示了基于拷贝写时复制内存分叉的亚毫秒级 VM 沙箱，能实现对不可信代码的近即时隔离和快速测试工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates sub-millisecond VM sandboxes built with Copy-On-Write memory forking. This enables near-instant isolation for running untrusted code or rapid test workflows, revolutionizing secure experimentation. Practical caveats include memory usage and kernel support.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示了基于拷贝写时复制内存分叉的亚毫秒级 VM 沙箱，能实现对不可信代码的近即时隔离和快速测试工作流。此技术对安全实验具有变革意义，但也需关注内存占用和内核支持程度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Moongate is a modern Ultima Online server emulator built in .NET 10 with Lua scripting, illustrating how legacy games can be reimagined with contemporary tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Moongate 是用 .NET 10 和 Lua 脚本构建的现代版 Ultima Online 服务器模拟器，展示了用现代工具重现经典游戏的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Moongate is a modern Ultima Online server emulator built in .NET 10 with Lua scripting, illustrating how legacy games can be reimagined with contemporary tooling. The project showcases rapid iteration, portability, and community-driven game hosting.</span><span class='zh'>Moongate 是用 .NET 10 和 Lua 脚本构建的现代版 Ultima Online 服务器模拟器，展示了用现代工具重现经典游戏的可能性。它体现了社区驱动的游戏托管、快速迭代和可移植性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Floci simulates AWS services locally, enabling offline development and testing without cloud access. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/robertcprice/nCPU" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A CPU that runs entirely on GPU</span><span class='zh'>一个完全在 GPU 上运行的 CPU</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A CPU implemented to run entirely on GPU hardware demonstrates offloading instruction scheduling and execution to GPU kernels, blurring the line between CPU and GPU architectures.</span><span class='zh'>这个在GPU上完整运行的CPU展示了将指令执行移植到GPU内核的可能性，模糊了CPU与GPU的边界。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A CPU implemented to run entirely on GPU hardware demonstrates offloading instruction scheduling and execution to GPU kernels, blurring the line between CPU and GPU architectures. The project is not about replacing CPUs yet, but it shows how GPU-centric designs could inform emulation, education, and certain HPC workloads. It hints at potential benefits like unified memory usage and high parallelism for specific tasks.</span><span class='zh'>这个在GPU上完整运行的CPU展示了将指令执行移植到GPU内核的可能性，模糊了CPU与GPU的边界。该项目尚不能替代实际CPU，但为仿真、教育和高性能计算场景提供了新的设计思路，可能带来内存整合和并行性提升的潜在收益。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/40710" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code runs Git reset –hard origin/main against project repo every 10 mins</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 每十分钟对项目仓库执行 git reset --hard origin/main</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code appears to run git reset --hard origin/main on a project every 10 minutes, wiping local changes and history.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 每 10 分钟就对一个项目执行 git reset --hard origin/main，导致本地改动与历史被清空。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code appears to run git reset --hard origin/main on a project every 10 minutes, wiping local changes and history. This illustrates the risks of high-frequency automation in code workflows and underscores the need for safeguards, review gates, and strict version-control discipline. The episode serves as a cautionary tale for AI-assisted coding tools.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 每 10 分钟就对一个项目执行 git reset --hard origin/main，导致本地改动与历史被清空。这揭示了高频自动化在代码工作流中的风险，强调需要设定保护措施、审查机制和严格的版本控制纪律。此事为 AI 辅助编码工具敲响警钟。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/03/29/top_stories_03292026'><span class='en'>Mar 29</span><span class='zh'>3月29日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>251</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47567969' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>195</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-47436592" data-id="47436592" data-tags="Programming|Web|Code" data-score="249" data-comments="100" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47436592" data-time="1773910416">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/antithesishq/bombadil" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bombadil: Property-based testing for web UIs</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil：Antithesis 的网页 UI 属性测试工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bombadil brings property-based testing to front-end UI testing, generating diverse interaction sequences to expose edge cases that example tests miss.</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil 为前端 UI 引入属性测试，通过自动生成各种交互序列来揭示常规测试难以覆盖的边界问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bombadil brings property-based testing to front-end UI testing, generating diverse interaction sequences to expose edge cases that example tests miss. It emphasizes automated shrinking of failing runs to minimal reproducing cases and integrates with common web stacks, helping teams catch flaky behavior before release.</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil 为前端 UI 引入属性测试，通过自动生成各种交互序列来揭示常规测试难以覆盖的边界问题。它还能对失败用例进行最小化缩减，便于定位复现，并与主流前端栈对接，帮助团队在发布前发现易出错的行为。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/alainnothere/llm-circuit-finder" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Duplicate 3 layers in a 24B LLM, logical deduction .22→.76. No training</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在24B 参数的 LLM 中复制三层提升推理能力</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN demonstrates duplicating three layers inside a 24B LLM to improve logical deduction by about 0.22–0.76 without any training.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了在一个 24B 参数的语言模型中复制三层的做法，未进行任何再训练就实现了逻辑推理能力的提升（约 0.22–0.76）。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN demonstrates duplicating three layers inside a 24B LLM to improve logical deduction by about 0.22–0.76 without any training. The work illustrates how targeted architectural tweaks can boost reasoning without changing data or parameters, highlighting ongoing exploration of model-internal mechanisms for reasoning.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了在一个 24B 参数的语言模型中复制三层的做法，未进行任何再训练就实现了逻辑推理能力的提升（约 0.22–0.76）。该工作突显通过内部网络结构调整来增强推理能力的潜力，推动对模型内部机制的探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/facebookresearch/hyperagents" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HyperAgents: Self-referential self-improving agents</span><span class='zh'>HyperAgents：自我指涉的自我提升代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HyperAgents introduces self-referential agents that can inspect and adjust their own policies to improve performance.</span><span class='zh'>HyperAgents 介绍了能够自我审视并调整自身策略的代理，形成自我提升循环。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HyperAgents introduces self-referential agents that can inspect and adjust their own policies to improve performance. The work explores auto-improvement loops, self-assessment, and the potential benefits and risks of self-modifying agents for complex tasks.</span><span class='zh'>HyperAgents 介绍了能够自我审视并调整自身策略的代理，形成自我提升循环。该研究探索自我评估与自我修改在解决复杂任务时的潜在收益与风险，以及对 AI 系统设计的影响。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">680</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/t8/hypura" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hypura – A storage-tier-aware LLM inference scheduler for Apple Silicon</span><span class='zh'>Hypura — 面向 Apple Silicon 的存储层感知型 LLM 推理调度器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hypura introduces a storage-tier-aware LLM inference scheduler designed for Apple Silicon, aiming to optimize memory usage and latency by intelligently staging data between RAM and storage.</span><span class='zh'>Hypura 引入面向 Apple Silicon 的存储层感知型 LLM 推理调度器，旨在通过在 RAM 与存储之间智能分层数据来优化内存使用与延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hypura introduces a storage-tier-aware LLM inference scheduler designed for Apple Silicon, aiming to optimize memory usage and latency by intelligently staging data between RAM and storage. By accounting for the different storage tiers, it reduces memory pressure during on-device inference of large models and can boost throughput on Macs.</span><span class='zh'>Hypura 引入面向 Apple Silicon 的存储层感知型 LLM 推理调度器，旨在通过在 RAM 与存储之间智能分层数据来优化内存使用与延迟。通过考虑不同存储层级，它在本地大模型推理中降低内存压力，并有望提升 Mac 上的吞吐量。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/03/24/top_stories_03242026'><span class='en'>Mar 24</span><span class='zh'>3月24日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>219</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504695' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>85</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-47350516" data-id="47350516" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Programming|Code" data-score="216" data-comments="121" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47350516" data-time="1773323352">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dune3d/dune3d" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dune3d: A parametric 3D CAD application</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d：一个参数化的3D CAD应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dune3d is an open-source parametric 3D CAD project that lets users define and modify geometry with parameters and constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d 是一个开源的参数化3D CAD项目，允许通过参数和约束来定义和修改几何形状。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dune3d is an open-source parametric 3D CAD project that lets users define and modify geometry with parameters and constraints. The project exemplifies how modular, scriptable CAD tooling could complement or challenge heavyweight CAD suites.</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d 是一个开源的参数化3D CAD项目，允许通过参数和约束来定义和修改几何形状。该项目展示了轻量、可脚本化的 CAD 工具如何挑战主流工具并实现更快速的试验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/elder-plinius/OBLITERATUS" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A tool that removes censorship from open-weight LLMs</span><span class='zh'>一个去除开源权重 LLM 审查的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This tool removes safety constraints from open-weight LLMs, enabling unrestricted generation but raising serious safety and abuse concerns.</span><span class='zh'>一个去除开源权重 LLM 审查的工具提高了生成自由度，但也带来严重安全与滥用担忧，凸显开放性与负责任部署之间的张力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This tool removes safety constraints from open-weight LLMs, enabling unrestricted generation but raising serious safety and abuse concerns. It underscores the tension between openness and responsible deployment of powerful models.</span><span class='zh'>一个去除开源权重 LLM 审查的工具提高了生成自由度，但也带来严重安全与滥用担忧，凸显开放性与负责任部署之间的张力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Proposes Verified Spec-Driven Development, a discipline combining formal specs with automated checks to improve correctness and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>提出经验证的规范驱动开发（VSDD），将形式化规范与自动检查结合以提升正确性与可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Proposes Verified Spec-Driven Development, a discipline combining formal specs with automated checks to improve correctness and reliability. It outlines steps from architecture to testing to verification for higher-assurance software.</span><span class='zh'>提出经验证的规范驱动开发（VSDD），将形式化规范与自动检查结合以提升正确性与可靠性。概述从架构到测试再到验证的流程，帮助团队实现更高保真度的软件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xodn348/han" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Han – A Korean programming language written in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Han——用 Rust 编写的韩语编程语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new language called Han is a Korean programming language implemented in Rust.</span><span class='zh'>一门名为 Han 的韩语编程语言由 Rust 实现，旨在用本地化的语法和工具链降低韩语使用者的学习门槛，同时受益于 Rust 的高性能。</span></p>

It explores localization of syntax and tooling, potentially lowering barriers for Korean speakers to learn programming, while benefiting from Rust&#x27;s performance.</span><span class='zh'>一门名为 Han 的韩语编程语言由 Rust 实现，旨在用本地化的语法和工具链降低韩语使用者的学习门槛，同时受益于 Rust 的高性能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/localstack/localstack" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Local Stack Archived their GitHub repo and requires an account to run</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 将仓库归档，运行需账户</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LocalStack has archived its GitHub repository and now requires you to sign in to run the software locally.</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 已将其 GitHub 仓库归档，现在运行需要账户登录。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LocalStack has archived its GitHub repository and now requires you to sign in to run the software locally. This marks a shift from open development toward a gated model, potentially affecting developers who rely on a free, self-hosted AWS-like environment. The move invites questions about accessibility and sustainability of community-focused open-source projects.</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 已将其 GitHub 仓库归档，现在运行需要账户登录。这标志着从开放开发向受限访问的转变，可能影响依赖免费本地 AWS 模拟环境的开发者。此举也引发关于社区开源项目可访问性与可持续性的讨论。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/03/23/top_stories_03232026'><span class='en'>Mar 23</span><span class='zh'>3月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>204</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47493657' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>121</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-47365597" data-id="47365597" data-tags="Politics|Legal|Code" data-score="202" data-comments="3" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47365597" data-time="1773414869">

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A renewed look at Meta’s lobbying, hidden political spending, and App Store accountability efforts shows how policy influence and platform governance intersect in tech regulation.</span><span class='zh'>对 Meta 游说、隐藏政治支出及 App Store 责任法案的新发现显示，政策影响力与平台治理在科技监管中的交汇。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rajko-horvat/OpenCiv1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenCiv1 – open-source rewrite of Civ1</span><span class='zh'>OpenCiv1：Civ1 的开源重写</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenCiv1 is an open-source rewrite of Civilization I, reimplementing its mechanics with modern code.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCiv1 是 Civilization I 的开源重写，使用现代代码重新实现其机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenCiv1 is an open-source rewrite of Civilization I, reimplementing its mechanics with modern code. It serves as a case study in retro game preservation and how modern tooling can re-create classics with improved modability and portability.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCiv1 是 Civilization I 的开源重写，使用现代代码重新实现其机制。它展示了通过现代工具对经典游戏进行保存与再创作，并提升了可修改性与跨平台性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/davidmonterocrespo24/velxio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Velxio 2.0 – Emulate Arduino, ESP32, and Raspberry Pi 3 in the Browser</span><span class='zh'>Velxio 2.0：在浏览器中模拟 Arduino、ESP32 与 Raspberry Pi 3</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Velxio 2.0 is a browser-based emulation tool that lets you run Arduino, ESP32, and Raspberry Pi 3 environments directly in the browser.</span><span class='zh'>Velxio 2.0 是一个基于浏览器的仿真工具，允许在浏览器中直接运行 Arduino、ESP32 和 Raspberry Pi 3 的环境。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Velxio 2.0 is a browser-based emulation tool that lets you run Arduino, ESP32, and Raspberry Pi 3 environments directly in the browser. It enables rapid prototyping, offline testing, and education, while highlighting current limitations of browser emulation.</span><span class='zh'>Velxio 2.0 是一个基于浏览器的仿真工具，允许在浏览器中直接运行 Arduino、ESP32 和 Raspberry Pi 3 的环境。它加速原型设计、离线测试与教学，同时也揭示了浏览器仿真的局限性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/russellromney/turbolite" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Turbolite – a SQLite VFS serving sub-250ms cold JOIN queries from S3</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Turbolite——将 SQLite VFS 与 S3 集成，实现冷数据下子 250ms 的 JOIN 查询</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Turbolite demonstrates a SQLite VFS that serves JOIN queries directly from S3 with sub-250ms cold-start latency.</span><span class='zh'>Turbolite 展示了一个将 SQLite VFS 与 S3 直接结合的方案，在冷启动下实现子 250ms 的 JOIN 查询。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Turbolite demonstrates a SQLite VFS that serves JOIN queries directly from S3 with sub-250ms cold-start latency. This approach enables fast analytical queries on object storage without a traditional DBMS, hinting at cost-efficient serverless data workflows and new caching strategies.</span><span class='zh'>Turbolite 展示了一个将 SQLite VFS 与 S3 直接结合的方案，在冷启动下实现子 250ms 的 JOIN 查询。该思路为对象存储上的快速分析查询提供了新路径，可能推动无服务器数据工作流和缓存策略的发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pyodide/pyodide" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pyodide: a Python distribution based on WebAssembly</span><span class='zh'>Pyodide：基于 WebAssembly 的 Python 发行版</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pyodide brings Python to the browser via WebAssembly, enabling data science stacks like NumPy, Pandas to run in the client.</span><span class='zh'>Pyodide 将 Python 通过 WebAssembly 运行在浏览器中，使前端直接运行 NumPy、Pandas 等数据科学工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pyodide brings Python to the browser via WebAssembly, enabling data science stacks like NumPy, Pandas to run in the client. It enables interactive notebooks and offline data experiments, lowering the barrier to high-level Python on the web.</span><span class='zh'>Pyodide 将 Python 通过 WebAssembly 运行在浏览器中，使前端直接运行 NumPy、Pandas 等数据科学工具。它为离线使用、教育示例和轻量级数据分析提供了新的前端能力，改变了在浏览器端进行 Python 开发的方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Rabrg/artificial-life" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artificial-life: A simple (300 lines of code) reproduction of Computational Life</span><span class='zh'>人工生命：一个简单的（300 行代码）计算生命的复现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This 300-line reproduction of Computational Life offers an approachable entry point into simple artificial-life simulations and emergent behavior.</span><span class='zh'>这个 300 行代码的人工生命复现提供了一个易于入门的简单仿生仿真案例，展示涌现行为的基本原理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This 300-line reproduction of Computational Life offers an approachable entry point into simple artificial-life simulations and emergent behavior.</span><span class='zh'>这个 300 行代码的人工生命复现提供了一个易于入门的简单仿生仿真案例，展示涌现行为的基本原理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Ataraxy-Labs/weave" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Weave – A language aware merge algorithm based on entities</span><span class='zh'>Weave——一种基于实体的语言感知合并算法</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Weave is a language-aware merge algorithm that uses entities to resolve conflicts during merges, prioritizing semantics over raw text.</span><span class='zh'>Weave 是一种基于实体的语言感知合并算法，在合并冲突时以实体语义为导向来解决分歧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Weave is a language-aware merge algorithm that uses entities to resolve conflicts during merges, prioritizing semantics over raw text. By aligning changes to underlying entities, it can reduce churn in collaborative editing and improve consistency across versions. The project could influence future diff/merge tooling for structured data.</span><span class='zh'>Weave 是一种基于实体的语言感知合并算法，在合并冲突时以实体语义为导向来解决分歧。通过将改动对齐到实体之上，能够降低协同编辑中的重复冲突并提升版本一致性。该项目可能影响未来对结构化数据的差异和合并工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RasKrebs/sonar" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Sonar – A tiny CLI to see and kill whatever&#x27;s running on localhost</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Sonar — 一个查看并终止本地运行服务的小巧 CLI</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sonar is a tiny command‑line utility that lists what&#x27;s listening on localhost and lets you kill offending processes quickly, speeding up developer workflows when ports are occupied.</span><span class='zh'>Sonar 是一个小巧的命令行工具，可列出本地监听端口并快速终止占用进程，方便在端口被占用时加速开发流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sonar is a tiny command‑line utility that lists what&#x27;s listening on localhost and lets you kill offending processes quickly, speeding up developer workflows when ports are occupied. It&#x27;s a pragmatic, cross‑platform tool for reclaiming ports without digging through process managers or GUI tools.</span><span class='zh'>Sonar 是一个小巧的命令行工具，可列出本地监听端口并快速终止占用进程，方便在端口被占用时加速开发流程。它以实用为主，跨平台地帮助开发者无需翻找复杂进程管理器即可回收端口。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mr-fatalyst/oxyde" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Oxyde – Pydantic-native async ORM with a Rust core</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Oxyde——基于 Pydantic 的原生异步 ORM，核心为 Rust</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Oxyde is a Python ORM that uses Pydantic for data validation and typing, with a Rust core delivering performance and safety.</span><span class='zh'>Oxyde 是一个 Python ORM，基于 Pydantic 进行数据校验和类型定义，核心用 Rust 实现以提升性能和安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Oxyde is a Python ORM that uses Pydantic for data validation and typing, with a Rust core delivering performance and safety. It aims to offer a modern, async-first ORM experience that competes with SQLAlchemy-style tooling, potentially changing how Python apps model and query data.</span><span class='zh'>Oxyde 是一个 Python ORM，基于 Pydantic 进行数据校验和类型定义，核心用 Rust 实现以提升性能和安全性。它强调异步优先的使用体验，可能对现有的 SQLAlchemy 等工具形成竞争，并改变 Python 应用的数据建模与查询方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/knowsuchagency/mcp2cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Mcp2cli – One CLI for every API, 96-99% fewer tokens than native MCP</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Mcp2cli——一条命令行即可对接所有 API，令牌消耗比原生 MCP 少 96–99%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>McP2cli abstracts multiple APIs behind a single CLI interface, enabling developers to call diverse services with fewer tokens.</span><span class='zh'>Mcp2cli 将多种 API 封装在一个统一的命令行接口下，开发者可用更少的 token 调用各类服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>McP2cli abstracts multiple APIs behind a single CLI interface, enabling developers to call diverse services with fewer tokens. The project promises dramatic token savings and a smoother developer experience, potentially accelerating automation workflows. It illustrates a trend toward universal CLI layers for heterogeneous APIs.</span><span class='zh'>Mcp2cli 将多种 API 封装在一个统一的命令行接口下，开发者可用更少的 token 调用各类服务。该项目承诺显著的令牌节省和更顺畅的开发体验，可能加速自动化工作流，体现了对异构 API 的通用 CLI 层的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gumyr/build123d" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Build123d: A Python CAD programming library</span><span class='zh'>Build123d：一个 Python CAD 编程库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Build123d is a Python-based CAD programming library that enables parametric, script-driven 3D modeling.</span><span class='zh'>Build123d 是一个用 Python 编写 CAD 的编程库，支持参数化、脚本驱动的三维建模。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Build123d is a Python-based CAD programming library that enables parametric, script-driven 3D modeling. By treating CAD as code, it aims to streamline automation, testing, and design exploration.</span><span class='zh'>Build123d 是一个用 Python 编写 CAD 的编程库，支持参数化、脚本驱动的三维建模。将 CAD 视为代码有助于实现自动化、测试和设计探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dbrll/ATTN-11" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Paper Tape Is All You Need – Training a Transformer on a 1976 Minicomputer</span><span class='zh'>纸带就够用——在1976年迷你计算机上训练 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A demonstration of training a Transformer on a 1976 minicomputer using paper tape and extremely limited memory.</span><span class='zh'>在1976年的迷你计算机上，靠纸带等极端受限的资源训练 Transformer。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A demonstration of training a Transformer on a 1976 minicomputer using paper tape and extremely limited memory. The project highlights how far AI tooling has evolved and what it takes to run modern models on vintage hardware, challenging assumptions about sets of resources required.</span><span class='zh'>在1976年的迷你计算机上，靠纸带等极端受限的资源训练 Transformer。这个演示凸显了 AI 工具与基础设施的飞跃，以及在复古硬件上运行现代模型所需的极端资源挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DenchHQ/DenchClaw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DenchClaw – Local CRM on Top of OpenClaw</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：DenchClaw——在 OpenClaw 之上的本地 CRM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DenchClaw is a local CRM built on top of OpenClaw, prioritizing offline-first operation and privacy-preserving data storage.</span><span class='zh'>DenchClaw 是一个基于 OpenClaw 的本地 CRM，强调离线优先与隐私友好的数据存储。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DenchClaw is a local CRM built on top of OpenClaw, prioritizing offline-first operation and privacy-preserving data storage. It demonstrates how open-source CRM tooling can be extended with local-first design, enabling small teams to manage customers without relying on cloud services.</span><span class='zh'>DenchClaw 是一个基于 OpenClaw 的本地 CRM，强调离线优先与隐私友好的数据存储。它展示了开源 CRM 工具如何通过本地优先设计扩展，让小型团队能够在不依赖云端的情况下高效管理客户。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ben-j-c/verilog2factorio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Verilog to Factorio Compiler and Simulator (Working RISC-V CPU)</span><span class='zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器与模拟器（可工作的 RISC-V CPU）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Verilog to Factorio compiler translates digital hardware descriptions into Factorio logic, enabling a working RISC-V CPU to be simulated inside the game.</span><span class='zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器把数字硬件描述转为 Factorio 逻辑，使游戏中的 RISC-V CPU 可被仿真。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Verilog to Factorio compiler translates digital hardware descriptions into Factorio logic, enabling a working RISC-V CPU to be simulated inside the game. This opens up a playful yet educational avenue for hardware prototyping, reveals performance constraints of software simulators, and demonstrates creative cross-domain tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器把数字硬件描述转为 Factorio 逻辑，使游戏中的 RISC-V CPU 可被仿真。这为硬件原型设计提供了一种有趣且具教育意义的方式，也揭示了软件仿真在性能上的限制，同时展示跨领域工具的创造力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vladich/pg_jitter" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Better JIT for Postgres</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 的更好 JIT</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pg_jitter proposes a faster JIT path for PostgreSQL, potentially boosting query speed on workloads with many generated queries.</span><span class='zh'>pg_jitter 提出在 PostgreSQL 上实现更快的 JIT 路径，可能提升对高并发和生成型查询的执行速度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pg_jitter proposes a faster JIT path for PostgreSQL, potentially boosting query speed on workloads with many generated queries. It highlights the ongoing tension between runtime code generation and maintainability, plus how JIT affects CPU cache and startup cost.</span><span class='zh'>pg_jitter 提出在 PostgreSQL 上实现更快的 JIT 路径，可能提升对高并发和生成型查询的执行速度。揭示了运行时代码生成与可维护性之间的平衡，以及 JIT 对 CPU 缓存和启动成本的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmElmo/proofshot" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: ProofShot – Give AI coding agents eyes to verify the UI they build</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: ProofShot – 让 AI 编码代理具备验证 UI 的能力</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ProofShot enables AI coding agents to inspect and verify the UI they generate, acting as a visual verifier for automated UI construction.</span><span class='zh'>ProofShot 让 AI 编码代理能够检查并验证它们生成的 UI，作为对自动化 UI 构建的可视化校验工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ProofShot enables AI coding agents to inspect and verify the UI they generate, acting as a visual verifier for automated UI construction. This reduces misalignment between intended design and rendered output, accelerating trusted AI-driven UI workflows. It showcases a practical approach to injecting human-in-the-loop feedback into automated UI pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>ProofShot 让 AI 编码代理能够检查并验证它们生成的 UI，作为对自动化 UI 构建的可视化校验工具。它减少了设计意图与最终呈现之间的错配，加速了 AI 驱动的 UI 工作流程中的信任建立。展示了将人类在环反馈引入自动化 UI 流程的实际方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/theredsix/agent-browser-protocol" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Open-source browser for AI agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：开源的 AI 代理浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open-source project defines a browser protocol and interface for running, visualizing, and interacting with autonomous AI agents inside the browser.</span><span class='zh'>该开源项目为在浏览器中运行、可视化和交互的自治 AI 代理定义了协议与界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open-source project defines a browser protocol and interface for running, visualizing, and interacting with autonomous AI agents inside the browser. By standardizing agent communications and providing a UI, it makes experimentation with multiple-agent setups and debugging easier for developers building agent-based systems.</span><span class='zh'>该开源项目为在浏览器中运行、可视化和交互的自治 AI 代理定义了协议与界面。通过规范代理通信并提供用户界面，它让开发者更方便地实验多代理配置并调试基于代理的系统。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Megadev is a development kit that enables modern tooling to develop for Sega Mega Drive and Mega CD, lowering the barrier to homebrew games and experiments on classic hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Megadev 是一个面向世嘉 Mega Drive 与 Mega CD 的开发套件，提供现代化的开发工具，降低在经典硬件上开发自制游戏与实验的门槛，通常包含硬件、固件与文档以实现测试与迭代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Megadev is a development kit that enables modern tooling to develop for Sega Mega Drive and Mega CD, lowering the barrier to homebrew games and experiments on classic hardware. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/creationix/rx" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RX – a new random-access JSON alternative</span><span class='zh'>RX：一种新的随机访问 JSON 替代方案</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RX offers a data format with random-access capabilities that aims to outperform JSON on certain workloads, enabling direct access to nested structures without full parsing.</span><span class='zh'>RX 提供具备随机访问能力的新的数据格式，旨在在某些工作负载上优于 JSON，实现对嵌套结构的直接访问而无需完整解析。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RX offers a data format with random-access capabilities that aims to outperform JSON on certain workloads, enabling direct access to nested structures without full parsing. It uses a binary layout with indexing and streaming semantics, suited for data intensive apps.</span><span class='zh'>RX 提供具备随机访问能力的新的数据格式，旨在在某些工作负载上优于 JSON，实现对嵌套结构的直接访问而无需完整解析。它采用二进制格式并带有索引与流式语义，适用于数据密集型应用，但需配套的工具链和设计模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jkool702/forkrun" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Forkrun – NUMA-aware shell parallelizer (50×–400× faster than parallel)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Forkrun — NUMA感知的Shell并行化器（比 parallel 快50–400倍）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Forkrun introduces NUMA-aware parallelization for shell commands, delivering dramatic speedups on multi-socket servers.</span><span class='zh'>Forkrun 提供对 shell 命令的 NUMA 感知并行化，在多插槽服务器上实现显著的速度提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Forkrun introduces NUMA-aware parallelization for shell commands, delivering dramatic speedups on multi-socket servers. The project demonstrates how memory locality and CPU affinity can unlock large performance gains for everyday CLI tasks. It could inspire a new wave of performance-focused shell tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Forkrun 提供对 shell 命令的 NUMA 感知并行化，在多插槽服务器上实现显著的速度提升。该项目展示了内存局部性与 CPU 亲和性如何为日常命令行任务带来巨大的性能提升，或将推动新一轮面向性能的 shell 工具设计。</span></p>

It demonstrates how developer tools can enforce privacy and scope in AI-assisted coding.</span><span class='zh'>nah 是一个面向 Claude Code 的上下文感知权限保护工具，能根据当前项目上下文限制 AI 的访问权限与提示信息。展示了在 AI 辅助编码中强化隐私与作用域控制的可行路径。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>QuickBEAM enables running JavaScript inside supervised Erlang/OTP processes, bridging the ergonomics of JS with the reliability of Erlang.</span><span class='zh'>QuickBEAM 使 JavaScript 可以在受监督的 Erlang/OTP 进程中运行，将 JS 的易用性与 Erlang 的可靠性结合起来，展示了在容错运行时内的跨语言协作模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>QuickBEAM enables running JavaScript inside supervised Erlang/OTP processes, bridging the ergonomics of JS with the reliability of Erlang. A glimpse into practical multi-language concurrency patterns.</span><span class='zh'>QuickBEAM 使 JavaScript 可以在受监督的 Erlang/OTP 进程中运行，将 JS 的易用性与 Erlang 的可靠性结合起来，展示了在容错运行时内的跨语言协作模式。提供了一种实用的多语言并发实现思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DavidHavoc/ayaFlow" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AyaFlow: A high-performance, eBPF-based network traffic analyzer written in Rust</span><span class='zh'>AyaFlow：用 Rust 编写的高性能基于 eBPF 的网络流量分析器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AyaFlow is a high-performance, Rust-based network traffic analyzer built on eBPF, enabling fast, safe, kernel-level visibility.</span><span class='zh'>AyaFlow 是一个用 Rust 编写、基于 eBPF 的高性能网络流量分析器，提供快速且安全的内核级可观测性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AyaFlow is a high-performance, Rust-based network traffic analyzer built on eBPF, enabling fast, safe, kernel-level visibility. It emphasizes memory safety and throughput for large-scale observability tasks. The project showcases Rust’s role in safe, efficient infrastructure tooling.</span><span class='zh'>AyaFlow 是一个用 Rust 编写、基于 eBPF 的高性能网络流量分析器，提供快速且安全的内核级可观测性。该工具强调内存安全与高吞吐，适合大规模观测任务，体现了 Rust 在基础设施工具中的应用潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/understudy-ai/understudy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Understudy – Teach a desktop agent by demonstrating a task once</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Understudy — 通过一次演示教会桌面自动化代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Understudy lets you demonstrate a task once and teach a desktop agent to repeat it across apps.</span><span class='zh'>Understudy 允许通过一次演示来教会桌面代理在跨应用中重复执行任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Understudy lets you demonstrate a task once and teach a desktop agent to repeat it across apps. This approach could dramatically accelerate building practical automation without scripting.</span><span class='zh'>Understudy 允许通过一次演示来教会桌面代理在跨应用中重复执行任务。这种方法可能极大地加速无脚本的实际自动化构建。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gritzko/librdx/tree/master/be" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Beagle, a source code management system that stores AST trees</span><span class='zh'>Beagle：存储 AST 树的源代码管理系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Beagle is a source-code management system that stores abstract syntax trees (ASTs) instead of plain text.</span><span class='zh'>Beagle 是一个以存储抽象语法树（AST）为核心的源代码管理系统，而非传统的文本存储。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Beagle is a source-code management system that stores abstract syntax trees (ASTs) instead of plain text. This enables language-aware diffs, safer refactoring, and cross-language tooling by operating on structural code representations. The approach promises deeper code intelligence than traditional diff-based VCS.</span><span class='zh'>Beagle 是一个以存储抽象语法树（AST）为核心的源代码管理系统，而非传统的文本存储。通过对代码结构的操作，可以实现语言感知的差异比较、更加安全的重构，以及跨语言的工具链协作。该方法在传统基于文本的版本控制之上带来更深层次的代码智能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/egmontkob/eb114294efbcd5adb1944c9f3cb5feda" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hyperlinks in terminal emulators</span><span class='zh'>终端模拟器中的超链接</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Terminal emulators can support clickable links via OSC 8, enabling direct navigation from CLI output.</span><span class='zh'>通过 OSC 8，终端模拟器可以实现可点击的链接，使 CLI 输出中的 URL 能直接跳转。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Terminal emulators can support clickable links via OSC 8, enabling direct navigation from CLI output. This capability improves developer workflows by making URLs and references actionable inside terminals. The note covers practical adoption, portability caveats, and user expectations.</span><span class='zh'>通过 OSC 8，终端模拟器可以实现可点击的链接，使 CLI 输出中的 URL 能直接跳转。此能力能提升开发工作流的效率与可用性，但在跨终端的可移植性与一致性方面也需注意。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dreddnafious/thereisnospoon" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>There is no spoon – A software engineers primer for demystified ML</span><span class='zh'>&quot;There is no spoon&quot;——软件工程师的去神秘化 ML 入门</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A software engineer-focused primer demystifies ML concepts, separating hype from fundamentals and providing practical mental models for integrating ML into software projects.</span><span class='zh'>&quot;There is no spoon&quot;——软件工程师的去神秘化 ML 入门，澄清 ML 的核心概念，并提供将 ML 融入软件项目的实用思维模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A software engineer-focused primer demystifies ML concepts, separating hype from fundamentals and providing practical mental models for integrating ML into software projects. It emphasizes intuition-driven debugging, data-centric thinking, and the distinction between model performance and product outcomes.</span><span class='zh'>&quot;There is no spoon&quot;——软件工程师的去神秘化 ML 入门，澄清 ML 的核心概念，并提供将 ML 融入软件项目的实用思维模型。强调以数据为核心的思考、直觉式调试，以及模型性能与产品结果之间的区别。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ppp-one/astra" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Astra: An open-source observatory control software</span><span class='zh'>Astra：一个开源的观测站/望远镜控制软件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astra is an open-source software package for observatory control, enabling telescope automation, instrument control, and remote operation.</span><span class='zh'>Astra 是一个开源的观测站/望远镜控制软件，支持望远镜自动化、仪器控制与远程操作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astra is an open-source software package for observatory control, enabling telescope automation, instrument control, and remote operation. By embracing open standards, it lowers the barrier to building affordable, shareable observatories and can accelerate amateur and research workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Astra 是一个开源的观测站/望远镜控制软件，支持望远镜自动化、仪器控制与远程操作。采用开放标准，有助于降低搭建可分享天文台的门槛，促进业余与科研工作流的协同。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Compresr-ai/Context-Gateway" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Context Gateway – Compress agent context before it hits the LLM</span><span class='zh'>Context Gateway：在进入 LLM 之前对上下文进行压缩</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Context Gateway introduces a pre-processing layer that compresses or summarizes agent context before sending prompts to an LLM, reducing token usage and latency.</span><span class='zh'>Context Gateway 在将上下文传给大型语言模型之前先进行压缩/摘要处理，减少令牌用量和延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Context Gateway introduces a pre-processing layer that compresses or summarizes agent context before sending prompts to an LLM, reducing token usage and latency. The approach can lower costs and improve privacy by trimming unnecessary information. It&#x27;s a practical lever for scalable AI assistants.</span><span class='zh'>Context Gateway 在将上下文传给大型语言模型之前先进行压缩/摘要处理，减少令牌用量和延迟。此方法有助于降低成本、提升隐私保护，并提升对话的可扩展性。为可扩展的 AI 助手提供了一个实用的优化点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cigrainger/duckdb-hnsw-acorn" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DuckDB community extension for prefiltered HNSW using ACORN-1</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：DuckDB 社区扩展，基于 ACORN-1 的预筛选 HNSW</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DuckDB gains a community extension that prefilters HNSW with ACORN-1 to speed up vector search inside analytics workloads.</span><span class='zh'>DuckDB 获得一个社区扩展，将 ACORN-1 的预筛选 HNSW 引入以加速分析工作负载中的向量检索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DuckDB gains a community extension that prefilters HNSW with ACORN-1 to speed up vector search inside analytics workloads. This keeps vectorized analytics fast without relying on external search services, benefiting ML and embedding-heavy workflows.</span><span class='zh'>DuckDB 获得一个社区扩展，将 ACORN-1 的预筛选 HNSW 引入以加速分析工作负载中的向量检索。无需外部搜索服务即可实现更快的向量分析，利于嵌入和机器学习密集型的工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/wolfssl/wolfguard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WolfGuard: WireGuard with FIPS 140-3 cryptography</span><span class='zh'>WolfGuard：带 FIPS 140‑3 加密的 WireGuard</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WolfGuard is a WireGuard-focused project that implements FIPS 140-3 cryptography, aligning VPN tech with government-grade security standards.</span><span class='zh'>WolfGuard 是一个聚焦于 WireGuard 的项目，实现了 FIPS 140-3 加密，确保 VPN 技术符合政府等级的安全标准。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WolfGuard is a WireGuard-focused project that implements FIPS 140-3 cryptography, aligning VPN tech with government-grade security standards. This makes it appealing for regulated industries or sensitive deployments, though it may come with certification overhead and potential compatibility considerations with standard WireGuard deployments.</span><span class='zh'>WolfGuard 是一个聚焦于 WireGuard 的项目，实现了 FIPS 140-3 加密，确保 VPN 技术符合政府等级的安全标准。对合规行业或敏感部署具有吸引力，但可能伴随认证成本和与常规 WireGuard 部署的兼容性挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security advisory reports a brief compromise in the Trivy ecosystem, prompting updates and coordinated responses for users to mitigate supply-chain risk.</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 生态系统短暂受损的安全公告促使用户更新并采取应对措施，以降低供应链风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security advisory reports a brief compromise in the Trivy ecosystem, prompting updates and coordinated responses for users to mitigate supply-chain risk. The incident underscores the fragility of open-source tooling and the need for rapid patching.</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 生态系统短暂受损的安全公告促使用户更新并采取应对措施，以降低供应链风险。事件凸显开源工具的脆弱性及快速修补的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/EverMind-AI/MSA" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MSA: Memory Sparse Attention</span><span class='zh'>MSA：记忆稀疏注意力</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MSA introduces memory-sparse attention to reduce memory and compute for long-context models, enabling scalable attention with less quadratic cost.</span><span class='zh'>MSA 提出记忆稀疏注意力，降低长上下文模型的内存与计算需求，使注意力计算更具可扩展性，或促使更大上下文的训练与推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MSA introduces memory-sparse attention to reduce memory and compute for long-context models, enabling scalable attention with less quadratic cost. This can impact how we design and train larger sequences.</span><span class='zh'>MSA 提出记忆稀疏注意力，降低长上下文模型的内存与计算需求，使注意力计算更具可扩展性，或促使更大上下文的训练与推理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bbatsov/neocaml" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neocaml – Rubocop Creator&#x27;s New OCaml Mode for Emacs</span><span class='zh'>Neocaml — Rubocop 创始人新建的 Emacs OCaml 模式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neocaml is an OCaml mode for Emacs from the creator of RuboCop, aiming to bring modern tooling into OCaml development.</span><span class='zh'>Neocaml 是 RuboCop 创始人推出的用于 Emacs 的 OCaml 模式，意在把现代开发工具带入 OCaml 开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neocaml is an OCaml mode for Emacs from the creator of RuboCop, aiming to bring modern tooling into OCaml development. It provides improved editing, navigation, and integration with OCaml tooling to streamline OCaml coding in Emacs.</span><span class='zh'>Neocaml 是 RuboCop 创始人推出的用于 Emacs 的 OCaml 模式，意在把现代开发工具带入 OCaml 开发。它提供更好的编辑、导航和与 OCaml 工具链的集成，帮助在 Emacs 中高效编写 OCAML 代码。</span></p>

It showcases scalable AI-assisted development workflows and the practical challenges of containerized automation.</span><span class='zh'>Optio 展示了在 Kubernetes 里编排 AI 编码代理，把一个工单推进成代码改动的 Pull Request。该项目体现了可扩展的 AI 辅助开发工作流及在容器化环境中实施自动化的实际挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ataraxy-labs/sem" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sem – Semantic version control. Entity-level diffs on top of Git</span><span class='zh'>Sem：语义版本控制，在 Git 之上实现实体级差异</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sem introduces semantic version control on top of Git, using entity-level diffs to track changes beyond plain text.</span><span class='zh'>Sem 在 Git 之上实现语义化版本控制，按实体级别记录差异，超越文本层面的变更。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sem introduces semantic version control on top of Git, using entity-level diffs to track changes beyond plain text. It aims to improve traceability, audits, and reproducibility for data- and structure-rich projects.</span><span class='zh'>Sem 在 Git 之上实现语义化版本控制，按实体级别记录差异，超越文本层面的变更。目标是提升对数据与结构丰富项目的可追溯性、审计与可复现性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/TechEmpower/FrameworkBenchmarks/issues/10932" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sunsetting the Techempower Framework Benchmarks</span><span class='zh'>停用 Techempower 框架基准</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Techempower Framework Benchmarks project is being sunset, ending active maintenance and updates.</span><span class='zh'>Techempower 框架基准项目将进入停止维护阶段，正式结束主动更新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Techempower Framework Benchmarks project is being sunset, ending active maintenance and updates. The move reflects the ongoing maintenance burden and evolving ecosystems, nudging developers to rely on maintained benchmarks or run their own tests while retaining historical performance insights.</span><span class='zh'>Techempower 框架基准项目将进入停止维护阶段，正式结束主动更新。此举反映出维护负担与生态变化的压力，促使开发者改为使用受维护的基准集或自行执行性能测试，同时保留历史性能快照作为参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/jjl/d998164191af59a594500687a679b98d" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Faster C software with Dynamic Feature Detection</span><span class='zh'>用动态特征检测提升 C 软件性能</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Shows how dynamic feature detection can enable faster C code by selecting optimized paths at run time, trading a bit of complexity for performance gains on diverse hardware.</span><span class='zh'>展示了动态特征检测如何通过在运行时选择优化路径来提升 C 代码性能，在不同硬件上换取性能提升，同时需权衡实现难度和可移植性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shows how dynamic feature detection can enable faster C code by selecting optimized paths at run time, trading a bit of complexity for performance gains on diverse hardware. Includes practical notes on implementation pitfalls and portability.</span><span class='zh'>展示了动态特征检测如何通过在运行时选择优化路径来提升 C 代码性能，在不同硬件上换取性能提升，同时需权衡实现难度和可移植性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jer-irl/threadprocs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Threadprocs – executables sharing one address space (0-copy pointers)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Threadprocs — 在同一地址空间共享可执行文件（零拷贝指针）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Threadprocs enables multiple executables to share a single address space, enabling zero-copy data sharing and fast IPC.</span><span class='zh'>Threadprocs 允许多个可执行文件共用一个地址空间，实现零拷贝的数据共享与快速进程间通信。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Threadprocs enables multiple executables to share a single address space, enabling zero-copy data sharing and fast IPC. The approach challenges traditional process isolation and could influence future runtime and OS design.</span><span class='zh'>Threadprocs 允许多个可执行文件共用一个地址空间，实现零拷贝的数据共享与快速进程间通信。这一思路挑战传统进程隔离，可能影响未来的运行时与操作系统设计。</span></p>
