<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cosmiciron/vmprint" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a zero-browser, pure-JS typesetting engine for bit-perfect PDFs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：零浏览器、纯 JavaScript 的位精确排版引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A pure-JS, zero-dependency typesetting engine that outputs bit-perfect PDFs without relying on a browser&#x27;s layout engine.</span><span class='zh'>一个纯 JavaScript、零依赖的排版引擎，输出位精确的 PDF，不依赖浏览器排版引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A pure-JS, zero-dependency typesetting engine that outputs bit-perfect PDFs without relying on a browser&#x27;s layout engine. It demonstrates how much you can push in the JS sandbox and opens up offline, deterministic printing workflows. This could inform reproducible publishing pipelines and minimal runtime footprints.</span><span class='zh'>一个纯 JavaScript、零依赖的排版引擎，输出位精确的 PDF，不依赖浏览器排版引擎。展示了在纯 JS 沙箱内还能实现的极致排版能力，并为离线、确定性打印工作流打开了可能性。这样的实现可能推动可重复出版流程和最小化运行时开销的探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openyak/desktop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenYak – An open-source Cowork that runs any model and owns your filesystem</span><span class='zh'>OpenYak：一个开源的协作工具，能运行任意模型并掌控你的文件系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenYak is an open-source coworking platform that can run any machine learning model and store data on the user&#x27;s own filesystem.</span><span class='zh'>OpenYak 是一个开源的协作平台，能够在本地运行任意模型并将数据存放在用户自有的文件系统中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenYak is an open-source coworking platform that can run any machine learning model and store data on the user&#x27;s own filesystem. Its architecture highlights data ownership and local experimentation, avoiding vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>OpenYak 是一个开源的协作平台，能够在本地运行任意模型并将数据存放在用户自有的文件系统中。此设计强调数据所有权和本地试验，避免对单一厂商的依赖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mistivia/lmp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lisp-style C++ template meta programming</span><span class='zh'>Lisp 风格的 C++ 模板元编程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Discusses using Lisp-like techniques in C++ template metaprogramming, leveraging functional styles and S-expression-inspired patterns to improve expressiveness and possibly reduce boilerplate.</span><span class='zh'>讨论在 C++ 模板元编程中应用 Lisp 风格的技巧，利用函数式风格和受 S 表达式启发的模式来提升表达力，或减轻样板代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Discusses using Lisp-like techniques in C++ template metaprogramming, leveraging functional styles and S-expression-inspired patterns to improve expressiveness and possibly reduce boilerplate.</span><span class='zh'>讨论在 C++ 模板元编程中应用 Lisp 风格的技巧，利用函数式风格和受 S 表达式启发的模式来提升表达力，或减轻样板代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/1st1/lat.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lat.md: Agent Lattice: a knowledge graph for your codebase, written in Markdown</span><span class='zh'>Lat.md：Agent Lattice——用 Markdown 编写的代码库知识图谱</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lat.md introduces Agent Lattice, a knowledge graph for your codebase expressed in Markdown.</span><span class='zh'>Lat.md 推出 Agent Lattice——用 Markdown 表达的代码库知识图谱，连接概念、 API 与架构，便于团队协作与文档自动化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lat.md introduces Agent Lattice, a knowledge graph for your codebase expressed in Markdown. It links concepts, APIs, and architecture in a human-friendly format, potentially aiding cross-team understanding and documentation automation. The approach favors approachable, readable knowledge graphs over heavy tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Lat.md 推出 Agent Lattice——用 Markdown 表达的代码库知识图谱，连接概念、 API 与架构，便于团队协作与文档自动化。该方法以可读性为优先，提供一种更易被团队采纳的知识图谱形式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/fastino-ai/GLiNER2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GLiNER2: Unified Schema-Based Information Extraction</span><span class='zh'>GLiNER2：统一的基于模式的信息抽取</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GLiNER2 extends unified schema-based information extraction, offering a common framework for pulling structured data across domains.</span><span class='zh'>GLiNER2 将统一的基于模式的信息抽取扩展到一个通用框架，支持跨领域提取结构化数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GLiNER2 extends unified schema-based information extraction, offering a common framework for pulling structured data across domains. By anchoring extractions to shared schemas, it simplifies data integration and improves model interoperability. The project aims to reduce bespoke extraction work and accelerate downstream analytics.</span><span class='zh'>GLiNER2 将统一的基于模式的信息抽取扩展到一个通用框架，支持跨领域提取结构化数据。通过将抽取结果锚定于共享的模式，简化数据集成并提高模型的互操作性。该项目旨在减少定制化抽取工作，加速后续分析流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xtellect/spaces" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A single-file C allocator with explicit heaps and tuning knobs</span><span class='zh'>单文件 C 分配器：显式堆和调优参数</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compact, single-file C memory allocator exposing explicit heaps and tunable knobs for fine-grained control.</span><span class='zh'>一个紧凑的单文件 C 内存分配器，提供显式堆和可调控的参数，允许对分配策略进行细粒度控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compact, single-file C memory allocator exposing explicit heaps and tunable knobs for fine-grained control. It serves as a practical reference for understanding allocator design, benchmarking, and embedding custom allocators without pulling in external libraries.</span><span class='zh'>一个紧凑的单文件 C 内存分配器，提供显式堆和可调控的参数，允许对分配策略进行细粒度控制。是理解分配器设计、基准测试与在项目中嵌入自定义分配器的实用参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vustagc/pianoterm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Pianoterm – Run shell commands from your Piano. A Linux CLI tool</span><span class='zh'>Pianoterm：通过钢琴按键运行 shell 命令的 Linux CLI 工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pianoterm maps shell commands to piano keys, letting users trigger terminal actions with a MIDI-like interface.</span><span class='zh'>Pianoterm 将 shell 命令映射到钢琴按键，使用户能够通过像 MIDI 一样的界面触发终端操作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pianoterm maps shell commands to piano keys, letting users trigger terminal actions with a MIDI-like interface. It&#x27;s a playful, practical example of periphery-driven productivity and could inspire more creative tooling around command-line workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Pianoterm 将 shell 命令映射到钢琴按键，使用户能够通过像 MIDI 一样的界面触发终端操作。这是对外设驱动生产力的一种趣味且实用的示例，或将激发更多命令行工作流的创意工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/manifold-systems/manifold/blob/master/docs/articles/binding_exprs.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rethinking Syntax: Binding by Adjacency</span><span class='zh'>重新思考语法：按相邻绑定</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposal to bind symbols by their adjacency in code rather than strict lexical or lexical scope.</span><span class='zh'>提出按代码临近性来绑定标识符，而非按严格作用域。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposal to bind symbols by their adjacency in code rather than strict lexical or lexical scope. The approach could simplify DSL design, improve composability, and affect compiler and analyzer implementations.</span><span class='zh'>提出按代码临近性来绑定标识符，而非按严格作用域。此思路或简化领域特定语言的设计、提升模块化，并影响编译器与静态分析实现。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces SpiceCrypt, a Python tool that decrypts LTspice-encrypted model files, enabling users to inspect and reuse proprietary models.</span><span class='zh'>SpiceCrypt 是一个 Python 库，可解密 LTspice 的加密模型文件，使用户能够查看或再利用专有模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces SpiceCrypt, a Python tool that decrypts LTspice-encrypted model files, enabling users to inspect and reuse proprietary models. The project lowers barriers for hardware researchers while raising questions about licensing and ethics.</span><span class='zh'>SpiceCrypt 是一个 Python 库，可解密 LTspice 的加密模型文件，使用户能够查看或再利用专有模型。该工具降低了处理传统 SPICE 数据的门槛，同时也引发许可与伦理方面的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/nwellnhof/libxml2-ee" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Libxml2 Enterprise Edition (AGPL, from the previous maintainer)</span><span class='zh'>Libxml2 企业版（来自前维护者，采用 AGPL 许可）</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Libxml2 Enterprise Edition is released under AGPL by the previous maintainer, raising licensing concerns for corporate users who rely on proprietary software with XML parsing.</span><span class='zh'>Libxml2 Enterprise Edition 由前维护者以 AGPL 许可发布，令依赖该库的企业用户对许可条款产生关注，因为 AGPL 在网络使用场景下要求公开源代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Libxml2 Enterprise Edition is released under AGPL by the previous maintainer, raising licensing concerns for corporate users who rely on proprietary software with XML parsing. The AGPL terms require sharing improvements when used over network, which affects embedded and SaaS deployments.</span><span class='zh'>Libxml2 Enterprise Edition 由前维护者以 AGPL 许可发布，令依赖该库的企业用户对许可条款产生关注，因为 AGPL 在网络使用场景下要求公开源代码。此举对嵌入式、SaaS 部署及商业使用的合规性产生影响，文中讨论了许可含义与社区分叉的潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RightNow-AI/autokernel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AutoKernel: Autoresearch for GPU Kernels</span><span class='zh'>AutoKernel：针对 GPU 内核的自动化研究工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AutoKernel automates search and tuning of GPU kernels, using autoresearch techniques to discover high‑performance implementations without manual CUDA optimization.</span><span class='zh'>AutoKernel 通过自动化搜索和调优 GPU 内核，利用自研究方法发现高性能实现，减少手工 CUDA 优化需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AutoKernel automates search and tuning of GPU kernels, using autoresearch techniques to discover high‑performance implementations without manual CUDA optimization. This can accelerate performance engineering and help developers get closer to peak hardware efficiency with less bespoke effort.</span><span class='zh'>AutoKernel 通过自动化搜索和调优 GPU 内核，利用自研究方法发现高性能实现，减少手工 CUDA 优化需求。该工具能加快性能工程工作，帮助开发者以更少定制化投入靠近硬件峰值效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/alefore/mini_svg/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Minimalist library to generate SVG views of scientific data</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用于生成科学数据 SVG 视图的极简库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces a tiny library that renders scientific data as SVG visuals, prioritizing simplicity and reproducibility over feature creep.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一个极简库，用于把科学数据渲染成 SVG 图表，强调简单、可重复的可视化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces a tiny library that renders scientific data as SVG visuals, prioritizing simplicity and reproducibility over feature creep. It suits quick plots for docs, dashboards, or educational demos, with easy integration into static pages.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一个极简库，用于把科学数据渲染成 SVG 图表，强调简单、可重复的可视化。适合快速文档、仪表盘或教学演示的静态页面集成。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lixiasky-back/coroTracer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I made a zero-copy coroutine tracer to find my scheduler&#x27;s lost wakeups</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我做了一个零拷贝的协程跟踪器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>I built a zero-copy coroutine tracer to detect missed wakeups in my scheduler, reducing the overhead of tracing while diagnosing latency issues.</span><span class='zh'>我做了一个零拷贝的协程跟踪器，用来定位调度器中丢失的唤醒，从而在不增加额外开销的情况下诊断延迟问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>I built a zero-copy coroutine tracer to detect missed wakeups in my scheduler, reducing the overhead of tracing while diagnosing latency issues. The approach helps pinpoint wakeup gaps without perturbing the system&#x27;s timing.</span><span class='zh'>我做了一个零拷贝的协程跟踪器，用来定位调度器中丢失的唤醒，从而在不增加额外开销的情况下诊断延迟问题。该方法在不打扰系统时序的前提下找到唤醒间隙。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GrobPaint positions itself as a lightweight image editor that sits between MS Paint’s simplicity and Paint.NET’s more advanced features.</span><span class='zh'>GrobPaint 定位为一款介于 MS Paint 的简易性和 Paint.NET 的进阶功能之间的轻量级绘图工具，提供易学又不失能力的绘图体验与现代化用户体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GrobPaint positions itself as a lightweight image editor that sits between MS Paint’s simplicity and Paint.NET’s more advanced features. The project can appeal to developers seeking a tiny, extensible tool with room to grow.</span><span class='zh'>GrobPaint 定位为一款介于 MS Paint 的简易性和 Paint.NET 的进阶功能之间的轻量级绘图工具，提供易学又不失能力的绘图体验与现代化用户体验。该项目对寻求小巧、可扩展且可成长工具的开发者具有吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/robotocore/robotocore" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Robotocore · a Digital Twin of AWS</span><span class='zh'>Robotocore：AWS 的数字孪生</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Robotocore provides a digital twin of AWS, enabling offline testing and simulation of cloud workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Robotocore 提供了 AWS 的数字孪生，便于在本地离线仿真云工作负载、测试基础设施变更，降低成本与风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Robotocore provides a digital twin of AWS, enabling offline testing and simulation of cloud workloads. By modeling cloud infrastructure as code, it helps operators prototype changes with reduced risk and cost.</span><span class='zh'>Robotocore 提供了 AWS 的数字孪生，便于在本地离线仿真云工作负载、测试基础设施变更，降低成本与风险。该开源项目推动云原生环境的本地化开发与试验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/adamveld12/ghost" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Intent-Based Commits</span><span class='zh'>基于意图的提交</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Intent-Based Commits advocates encoding developer intent into commit messages and history, enabling richer reviews and easier future changes beyond line-by-line diffs.</span><span class='zh'>基于意图的提交强调在提交信息与历史中体现开发者意图，而非逐行差异，以促进更深层次的代码审查和未来变更的可维护性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Intent-Based Commits advocates encoding developer intent into commit messages and history, enabling richer reviews and easier future changes beyond line-by-line diffs. It hints at a future where version control semantics evolve toward higher-level programming constructs.</span><span class='zh'>基于意图的提交强调在提交信息与历史中体现开发者意图，而非逐行差异，以促进更深层次的代码审查和未来变更的可维护性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/YuminosukeSato/ironkernel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How HN: Ironkernel – Python expressions, Rust parallel</span><span class='zh'>Ironkernel：Python 表达式、Rust 并行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ironkernel is a project combining Python-style expressions with Rust-backed parallel execution to speed up evaluation tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Ironkernel 项目将 Python 风格的表达式与 Rust 的并行执行结合，提升表达式计算的性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ironkernel is a project combining Python-style expressions with Rust-backed parallel execution to speed up evaluation tasks. It explores language design and performance trade-offs between dynamic expression evaluation and compiled performance.</span><span class='zh'>Ironkernel 项目将 Python 风格的表达式与 Rust 的并行执行结合，提升表达式计算的性能。它讨论了动态表达式语言与编译型性能之间的权衡以及实现细节。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dbslice lets you extract a realistic, small slice of a production database to reproduce bugs in staging, enabling reliable debugging with safer data handling.</span><span class='zh'>Dbslice 允许从生产数据库提取一个小规模的、具备真实数据特征的切片，在预发布环境中复现缺陷，提升调试的可重复性并保护数据隐私。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/nolasoft/okgit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Effective Git</span><span class='zh'>有效的 Git</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Showcases a practical Git workflow or tool called Effective Git that helps teams optimize version control practices.</span><span class='zh'>展示一个名为 Effective Git 的实用工作流/工具，帮助团队优化版本控制实践。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Showcases a practical Git workflow or tool called Effective Git that helps teams optimize version control practices. It covers strategies for commit hygiene, branching, rebasing, and collaboration to minimize friction and maximize velocity.</span><span class='zh'>展示一个名为 Effective Git 的实用工作流/工具，帮助团队优化版本控制实践。涵盖提交规范、分支、变基和协作策略，以减少摩擦、提升开发速度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The human.json Protocol defines a simple JSON-based format for representing human-centric data, enabling interoperable data exchanges and annotations.</span><span class='zh'>human.json 协议定义了一种用于表示人类相关数据的简易 JSON 格式，便于互操作和标注信息的交换。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/QAInsights/AIx" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Quillx is an open standard for disclosing AI involvement in software projects</span><span class='zh'>Quillx 开放标准：披露软件项目中的 AI 参与</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Presents Quillx, an open standard for disclosing AI involvement in software projects, enabling transparency about machine-generated contributions and AI-assisted components.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Quillx，一项用于披露软件项目中 AI 参与的开放标准，旨在提升对机器生成贡献和 AI 辅助组件的透明度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Presents Quillx, an open standard for disclosing AI involvement in software projects, enabling transparency about machine-generated contributions and AI-assisted components. The aim is to foster trust, accountability, and clearer licensing in human-AI workflows.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Quillx，一项用于披露软件项目中 AI 参与的开放标准，旨在提升对机器生成贡献和 AI 辅助组件的透明度。助力建立信任、问责以及更清晰的混合人机工作流程的许可边界。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/CSMWrap/CSMWrap" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CSMWrap: Legacy BIOS booting on UEFI-only systems via SeaBIOS</span><span class='zh'>CSMWrap：通过 SeaBIOS 在仅 UEFI 的系统上引导传统 BIOS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CSMWrap enables legacy BIOS booting on UEFI-only systems using SeaBIOS, helping compatibility for older operating systems or bootloaders.</span><span class='zh'>CSMWrap 通过 SeaBIOS 使在仅有 UEFI 的系统上实现传统 BIOS 引导成为可能，提升对旧系统或引导器的兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CSMWrap enables legacy BIOS booting on UEFI-only systems using SeaBIOS, helping compatibility for older operating systems or bootloaders. It documents setup, limitations, and potential performance or security considerations.</span><span class='zh'>CSMWrap 通过 SeaBIOS 使在仅有 UEFI 的系统上实现传统 BIOS 引导成为可能，提升对旧系统或引导器的兼容性。文中讲解了安装/配置要点、局限性以及潜在的性能与安全考虑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rhodey/hecate" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hecate – Call an AI from Signal</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Hecate — 通过 Signal 调用 AI</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hecate lets users call an AI from within Signal, opening new pathways for private AI interactions within messaging apps.</span><span class='zh'>Hecate 允许在 Signal 中直接调用 AI，开启在端对端加密的聊天环境中对 AI 的新访问路径。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hecate lets users call an AI from within Signal, opening new pathways for private AI interactions within messaging apps. The project demonstrates how AI backends can be accessed from end-to-end encrypted chats, raising questions about privacy, latency, and bot capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>Hecate 允许在 Signal 中直接调用 AI，开启在端对端加密的聊天环境中对 AI 的新访问路径。该项目展示了将 AI 后端接入即时通讯应用的可行性，同时也引发关于隐私、时延和机器人能力的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux community reports ambient light sensor support on M2 Macs, enabling automatic brightness control under Linux.</span><span class='zh'>Asahi Linux 社区称在 M2 MacBook 上的环境光传感器已工作，可以在 Linux 下实现自动亮度调节。</span></p>
